+
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.002#8 — durable and ephemeral seats receive the same static clause for every private cause.
+#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
+pub fn seat_refusal(schema:&Schema,error:&RuntimeError,viewer:&str)->Refusal {
+    let mut why=error.refusal();
+    if viewer.starts_with("service:") && let RuntimeError::Rule {table,effect}|RuntimeError::AuthorizationBudget {table,effect}=error {
+        if let Some(def)=schema.table(table) {why.message.push_str(&format!("; {table} rule clause: {effect} <- {}",def.rules.for_effect(*effect).quarry()));}
+    }
+    why
+}

diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/cascade_repair.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/cascade_repair.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..de563fb355
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/cascade_repair.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,56 @@
+//! A fitting recipe for the whole delete, evaluated with the engine's prices.
+use snapback4_core::schema::{Schema, Constraint, references::{OnDelete, Price}};
+use std::collections::BTreeSet;
+
+pub(super) fn reaches(schema:&Schema,root:&str,target:&str)->bool {
+    let mut seen=BTreeSet::from([root.to_owned()]);let mut todo=vec![root.to_owned()];
+    while let Some(parent)=todo.pop() {
+        if parent==target {return true;}
+        for r in schema.referencing(&parent) {if r.action==OnDelete::Cascade && seen.insert(r.child.into()) {todo.push(r.child.into());}}
+    }
+    false
+}
+
+// @ref LLP 3000.039#7 — caps share one delete reservation, never a per-child allowance.
+pub(super) fn recipe(schema: &Schema, parent: &str, job: bool) -> String {
+    let mut targets = BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut reached = BTreeSet::from([parent.to_owned()]);
+    let mut todo = vec![parent.to_owned()];
+    while let Some(table) = todo.pop() {
+        for r in schema.referencing(&table) {
+            if r.action.writes() { targets.insert((r.child.to_owned(), r.column.to_owned())); }
+            if r.action == OnDelete::Cascade && reached.insert(r.child.to_owned()) { todo.push(r.child.to_owned()); }
+        }
+    }
+    let candidate = |cap: u32| {
+        let mut trial = schema.clone();
+        for (child, column) in &targets {
+            // A unique reference already has the tightest possible bound.
+            if trial.reference_cap(child, column) == Some(1) { continue; }
+            let table = trial.tables.get_mut(child).unwrap();
+            let mut found = false;
+            for constraint in &mut table.constraints {
+                if let Constraint::Cap { by, max } = constraint && by.len() == 1 && &by[0] == column {
+                    *max = (*max).min(cap); found = true;
+                }
+            }
+            if !found { table.constraints.push(Constraint::Cap { by: vec![column.clone()], max: cap }); }
+        }
+        trial.prepare_write_reservations();
+        let price = trial.delete_total_reservation(parent)?;
+        Some((trial, price))
+    };
+    let fits = |price: &Price| super::reference_actions::over_ceiling(price, job).is_none();
+    let fallback = format!("keep `on delete restrict` and delete the child rows first in a job (`for row in <child>:`), then delete the {parent} row; choose caps only if they are product limits (guide writes)");
+    if !candidate(1).is_some_and(|(_, price)| fits(&price)) { return format!("even cap 1 cannot fit this delete's fixed heap/row reservation; {fallback}"); }
+    let (mut low, mut high) = (1, 500);
+    while low < high {
+        let mid = low + (high - low + 1) / 2;
+        if candidate(mid).is_some_and(|(_, price)| fits(&price)) { low = mid; } else { high = mid - 1; }
+    }
+    let (trial, price) = candidate(low).unwrap();
+    let unique=targets.iter().map(|(child,column)|format!("`unique {column}` on {child}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" or ");
+    let caps = targets.iter().map(|(child, column)| format!("`cap {} by {column}` on {child}", trial.reference_cap(child, column).unwrap())).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
+    let limits = if job { snapback4_core::Ceilings::job() } else { snapback4_core::Ceilings::default() };
+    format!("apply {caps} together: the whole delete (including the root) reserves {} / {} rows written and {} / {} heap bytes, with all indexes, derived writes and event images priced together. A count-maintained source commonly reserves 3 × its rows (the row plus its old and new aggregate groups), not one row per child; for a one-to-one relation use {unique} instead; {fallback}", price[3], limits.rows_written, price[4], limits.heap_bytes)
+}

+    notes.extend(write_affordances::notes(&catalog, &schema));

     notes.extend(columns::notes(&catalog, &schema));

+        for note in &mut notes {

+                note.message.insert_str(0,"provisional while E_SYNC is unresolved: ");
+            }
+        }
+    }
     // @ref LLP 3000.032#4.3 — use the expanded mapping, never guess default operation names.
     let receipt_members = catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|doc| {
         let module = std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy();
diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/owner_equality.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/owner_equality.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..e558a87aac
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/owner_equality.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,97 @@
+//! Advisory proof of a child owner copied and checked against a stable parent.
+use super::catalog::Catalog;
+use crate::ast::{self, Expr, ReadExpr, ShapeField};
+use snapback4_core::schema::{Schema, Table, Constraint, ColumnType, RuleExpr, RuleTerm};
+use std::collections::BTreeSet;
+fn stable(table: &Table, fields: &[&str]) -> bool {
+    if fields.iter().any(|field|table.written_by.contains_key(*field)||table.maintained.contains_key(*field)) { return false; }
+    matches!(table.rules.update, RuleExpr::Deny) || fields.iter().all(|field| table.constraints.iter().any(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Immutable { fields } if fields.iter().any(|f| f == field))))
+}
+fn conjuncts(rule: &RuleExpr) -> Vec<&RuleExpr> { match rule { RuleExpr::And(rs) => rs.iter().flat_map(conjuncts).collect(), r => vec![r] } }
+fn equality(rule: &RuleExpr, a: &RuleTerm, b: &RuleTerm) -> bool {
+    conjuncts(rule).iter().any(|r| matches!(r, RuleExpr::Eq(x,y) if x == a && y == b || x == b && y == a))
+}
+fn rename(rule: &mut RuleExpr, child: &str, parent: &str) {
+    let term = |t: &mut RuleTerm| if matches!(t, RuleTerm::Field(f) if f == child) { *t = RuleTerm::Field(parent.into()); };
+    match rule {
+        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b) | RuleExpr::Ne(a,b) | RuleExpr::Compare(_,a,b) => { term(a); term(b); }
+        RuleExpr::And(rs) | RuleExpr::Or(rs) => for r in rs { rename(r,child,parent); },
+        RuleExpr::Not(r) => rename(r,child,parent),
+        RuleExpr::Exists { key,equalities,.. } | RuleExpr::Created { key,equalities,.. } => { for k in key { term(k); } for (_,v) in equalities { term(v); } },
+        _ => {}
+    }
+}
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.002#7 — no application assertion alone verifies stored child ownership.
+pub(super) fn readable(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &Schema, read: &ReadExpr, known: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
+    let Some(child) = schema.table(&read.table) else { return false; };
+    if schema.maintain_for(&read.table).is_some() || system_writes(catalog,&read.table) { return false; }
+    for part in &read.keys {
+        let Some(reference) = &part.column else { continue; };
+        let Some(ColumnType::Ref { table: parent_name }) = child.columns.get(reference) else { continue; };
+        let Some(parent) = schema.table(parent_name) else { continue; };
+        // Expiry fires no delete action; every expiring parent can lose its identity.
+        if parent.expire.is_some() || parent.ephemeral.is_some() { continue; }
+        // Immutable owner fields do not prevent a kept id being deleted and recreated.
+        if child.on_delete.get(reference).is_none_or(|a| *a==snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Keep)
+            && (!matches!(parent.rules.delete,RuleExpr::Deny)
+                || parent.on_delete.values().any(|a| *a==snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Cascade)) { continue; }
+        if schema.maintain_for(parent_name).is_some() || system_writes(catalog,parent_name) { continue; }
+        let input = crate::print::expression(&part.value);
+        let known_parent = known.contains(&format!("exists {parent_name}[id = {input}]"));
+        let projected_parent = matches!(&part.value, Expr::Member { base,name,.. } if name == "id" && matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal { .. }))
+            && projected_from(catalog, read, parent_name);
+        if !known_parent && !projected_parent { continue; }
+        for (owner, kind) in &child.columns {
+            if *kind != ColumnType::Principal || !stable(child, &[reference,owner]) { continue; }
+            for (parent_owner, kind) in &parent.columns {
+                if *kind != ColumnType::Principal || !stable(parent, &[parent_owner]) { continue; }
+                let a = RuleTerm::Field(owner.clone());
+                let b = RuleTerm::Reference { table: parent_name.clone(), column: reference.clone(), field: parent_owner.clone(), old:false };
+                let verified = child.rules.insert.disjuncts().iter().all(|branch| equality(branch,&a,&b)
+                    || equality(branch,&a,&RuleTerm::Viewer) && equality(branch,&b,&RuleTerm::Viewer));
+                if !verified || matches!(child.rules.insert,RuleExpr::Deny) { continue; }
+                if child.rules.read.any_term(&|t| matches!(t,RuleTerm::Field(f) if f!=owner)) || parent.rules.read.any_term(&|t| matches!(t,RuleTerm::Field(f) if f!=parent_owner)) { continue; }
+                let mut readers = child.rules.read.clone(); rename(&mut readers,owner,parent_owner);
+                if readers == parent.rules.read { return true; }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    false
+}
+fn system_writes(catalog:&Catalog,table:&str)->bool {
+    catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.programs).filter(|p|p.kind==ast::ProgramKind::Job).any(|p| {
+        if p.native { return true; }
+        let mut changed=false;let mut body=p.prelude.clone();body.extend(p.body.clone());
+        super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body,&mut |e| if matches!(e,Expr::Write { table:t,.. } if t==table) || matches!(e,Expr::Call { .. }) { changed=true; });
+        changed
+    })
+}
+fn projected_from(catalog: &Catalog, wanted: &ReadExpr, parent: &str) -> bool {
+    let mut found = false;
+    for doc in &catalog.documents {
+        for p in &doc.programs {
+            let mut body = p.body.clone();
+            super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body, &mut |expr| {
+                if let Expr::Shape { base,fields,.. } = expr && matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Read(r) if r.table == parent) {
+                    // Only the direct field's probe has this focal row. Nested shapes do not.
+                    found |= fields.iter().any(|f| matches!(f,ShapeField::Field { value:Expr::Exists { read,.. },.. } if read.site == wanted.site));
+                }
+            });
+        }
+    }
+    found
+}
+/// Positional typed table arguments denote id, even beside other unique keys.
+pub(super) fn typed_id(catalog: &Catalog, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
+    let [part] = read.keys.as_slice() else { return false; };
+    let Expr::Name { name,.. } = &part.value else { return false; };
+    for doc in &catalog.documents {
+        for p in &doc.programs {
+            if !p.args.iter().any(|a| a.name == *name && matches!(&a.type_.kind,ast::TypeKind::Ref { table } if table == &read.table)) { continue; }
+            let mut seen = false; let mut body=p.body.clone();
+            super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body,&mut |e| if matches!(e,Expr::Read(r) if r.site == read.site) { seen=true; });
+            if seen { return true; }
+        }
+    }
+    false
+}

 pub(super) fn candidates<'a>(info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, types: &[(Ty, bool)], synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>) -> Vec<&'a IndexDecl> {
     let identity = read.index.is_none() && types.len() == 1 &&
-        (types[0].0.required() == Ty::Id || types[0].0.required() == Ty::Ref(read.table.clone()));
+        (types[0].0.required() == Ty::Id || types[0].0.required() == Ty::Ref(read.table.clone())
+            || (!types[0].0.is_optional() && matches!(types[0].0.required(),Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==read.table)));
     info.indexes.iter().filter(|index| !synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())))
         .filter(|index| match &read.index {
             Some(name) => &index.name == name,
@@ -17,7 +18,7 @@ pub(super) fn candidates<'a>(info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, types: &[(Ty,
             let expected = if index.computed.contains_key(column) { Ty::Int }
                 else if column == "id" { Ty::Ref(read.table.clone()) }
                 else { info.columns.get(column).map(Ty::from_scalar).unwrap_or(Ty::Unknown) };
-            ty.comparable(&expected) || (*literal && expected.is_identity())
+            ty.comparable(&expected) || (!ty.is_optional() && matches!((ty.required(),expected.required()),(Ty::Row(a)|Ty::WriteRow(a),Ty::Ref(b)) if a==b)) || (*literal && expected.is_identity())
         })).collect()
 }
 
diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program.rs
index fb68072a9c..388b714c55 100644
--- a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program.rs
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program.rs
@@ -348,6 +348,10 @@ impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
     // ----- reads -----
 
     fn read(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {

diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/expr.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/expr.rs
index e18ceb663e..aa7fa17fce 100644
--- a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/expr.rs
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/expr.rs
@@ -624,6 +624,23 @@ impl Binder<'_> {
         self.expr(expr, out)
     }
 
+    pub(super) fn expr_as_reference(&mut self, expr: &A, out: &mut setup::Body, expected: &Ty) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
+        let (value, ty) = self.expr_as_identity(expr, out, expected)?;
+        Ok(self.row_identity(value, ty, expected))
+    }
+
+    // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#8 — contextual projection is exactly `.id`,
+    // without a lookup; optional and cross-table rows still need an edit.
+    pub(super) fn row_identity(&self, value: Expr, ty: Ty, expected: &Ty) -> (Expr, Ty) {
+        if !ty.is_optional() && matches!(ty.required(), Ty::Row(_) | Ty::WriteRow(_)) {
+            let table = match ty.required() { Ty::Row(t) | Ty::WriteRow(t) => t, _ => unreachable!() };
+            if expected.required() == Ty::Ref(table.clone()) {
+                return (ir::field(value, "id"), Ty::Ref(table));
+            }
+        }
+        (value, ty)
+    }
+
     pub(super) fn call(&mut self, name: &str, args: &[A], site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
         if let Some(result) = self.job_query_call(name, args, site, out) { return result; }
         // Compiler-only references bypass the function catalog entirely.

+//! Advisory mismatches between a program's caller gate and its table rule.
+use super::catalog::Catalog;
+use crate::{ast::*, Diagnostic};
+use snapback4_core::schema::{Schema, RuleExpr, RuleTerm};
+use std::collections::BTreeMap;
+fn seat(expr: &Expr) -> Option<&str> {
+    let Expr::Binary { op,left,right,.. }=expr else { return None; };
+    if op == "and" { return seat(left).or_else(|| seat(right)); }
+    if op != "=" { return None; }
+    let name = |e: &Expr| matches!(e, Expr::Name { name,.. } if name == "viewer");
+    let value = if name(left) { right.as_ref() } else if name(right) { left.as_ref() } else { return None; };
+    match value { Expr::Literal { value:Literal::Str(s),.. } if s.starts_with("service:") => Some(s), _=>None }
+}
+fn fields(rule: &RuleExpr) -> Vec<String> {
+    match rule {
+        RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(f))|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Viewer)=>vec![f.clone()],
+        RuleExpr::And(rs)|RuleExpr::Or(rs)=>rs.iter().flat_map(fields).collect(),
+        _=>vec![]
+    }
+}
+fn required_owners(rule:&RuleExpr)->Vec<String> {
+    match rule {
+        RuleExpr::And(parts)=>parts.iter().flat_map(required_owners).collect(),
+        RuleExpr::Or(parts)=>{
+            let Some(first)=parts.first() else {return vec![]};
+            let mut common=required_owners(first);
+            for part in &parts[1..] {let have=required_owners(part);common.retain(|f|have.contains(f));}
+            common
+        }
+        other=>fields(other),
+    }
+}
+fn admits_seat(rule: &RuleExpr, seat: &str) -> bool {
+    match rule {
+        RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Public { .. }=>true,
+        RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Literal(v))|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(v),RuleTerm::Viewer)=>v.as_str().ok()==Some(seat),
+        RuleExpr::Or(rs)=>rs.iter().any(|r|admits_seat(r,seat)),
+        _=>false
+    }
+}
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.002#7 — these notes state conditional argument requirements, never an impossibility theorem over dynamic inputs.
+pub(super) fn notes(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &Schema) -> Vec<Diagnostic> {
+    let mut out=Vec::new();
+    for doc in &catalog.documents {
+        for table in &doc.tables {
+            for column in &table.columns {
+                if column.type_.kind==TypeKind::Principal && let Some(Expr::Literal {value:Literal::Str(value),..})=&column.default
+                    && catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.personas).any(|p|p==value) {
+                    out.push(Diagnostic::new(&column.site,"N_PERSONA_PRINCIPAL",format!("{}.{} defaults to bare persona '{value}', whose dev principal is 'dev:{value}'",table.name,column.name),format!("use `default 'dev:{value}'` for development, or supply the authenticated principal in the mutation for production")).guide("personas"));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        for program in &doc.programs {
+            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Query || program.native { continue; }
+            body(catalog,schema,program,&program.body,None,&mut out);
+        }
+    }
+    out
+}
+fn body(catalog:&Catalog,schema:&Schema,program:&ProgramDecl,statements:&[Stmt], mut gate:Option<String>,out:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
+    let mut lookups: BTreeMap<String,(String,Vec<String>)>=BTreeMap::new();
+    for statement in statements {
+        if let Stmt::Require { condition,code,site,.. }=statement {
+            if let Some(seat)=seat(condition) { gate=Some(seat.into()); }
+            for (alias,(table,owners)) in &lookups {
+                let mut uses_owner=false; let mut copy=condition.clone();
+                super::intent_walk::expression(&mut copy,&mut |e| if let Expr::Member { base,name,.. }=e && matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Name { name,.. } if name==alias) && owners.contains(name) { uses_owner=true; });
+                if uses_owner && code != "NOT_FOUND" {
+                    out.push(Diagnostic::new(site,"N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN",format!("{}: callers who cannot read {table} get NOT_FOUND at `{alias}`'s lookup, never {code} from this require",program.name),
+                        format!("put a caller authorization check before the lookup if {code} must win; otherwise keep NOT_FOUND as the privacy-preserving answer and test it (guide rules)")).guide("rules"));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        if let Stmt::Bind { names,value:Expr::Assert { value,code,.. },.. }=statement && code=="NOT_FOUND" && let Expr::Read(read)=value.as_ref() {
+            if let [name]=names.as_slice() && let Some(table)=schema.table(&read.table) {
+                let owners=required_owners(&table.rules.read);
+                if !owners.is_empty() { lookups.insert(name.clone(),(read.table.clone(),owners)); }
+            }
+        }
+        let mut copy=vec![statement.clone()];
+        // Inspect only this statement's expressions; nested control flow receives its own gate below.
+        if !matches!(statement,Stmt::If { .. }|Stmt::For { .. }|Stmt::As { .. }) {
+            super::intent_walk::statements(&mut copy,&mut |expr| {
+                let Expr::Write { op,table,fields:payload,site,.. }=expr else { return; };
+                let Some(def)=schema.table(table) else { return; };
+                for field in payload.iter() {
+                    let principal=catalog.tables.get(table).and_then(|t|t.columns.get(&field.name)).is_some_and(|t|t.kind==TypeKind::Principal);
+                    if principal && let Some(Expr::Literal { value:Literal::Str(value),.. })=&field.value
+                        && catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.personas).any(|p| p==value) {
+                        out.push(Diagnostic::new(&field.site,"N_PERSONA_PRINCIPAL",format!("{table}.{} stores bare persona '{value}', but its dev viewer is 'dev:{value}'",field.name),format!("write 'dev:{value}' for development, or an authenticated account principal in production; personas are names, not principals")).guide("personas"));
+                    }
+                }
+                let Some(seat)=gate.as_deref() else { return; };
+                let effect=match op { WriteOp::Insert=>"insert",WriteOp::Update=>"update", _=>return };
+                let rule=if effect=="insert" { &def.rules.insert } else { &def.rules.update };
+                if admits_seat(rule,seat) { return; }
+                let others=required_owners(rule).into_iter().filter(|owner| payload.iter().find(|f|&f.name==owner).map_or(effect=="update",|f| {
+                    let value=f.value.clone().unwrap_or_else(||Expr::Name { name:f.name.clone(),site:f.site.clone() });
+                    !matches!(value,Expr::Name { ref name,.. } if name=="viewer") && !matches!(value,Expr::Literal { value:Literal::Str(ref s),.. } if s==seat)
+                })).collect::<Vec<_>>();
+                if others.is_empty() { return; }
+                out.push(Diagnostic::new(site,"N_WRITE_DENIED",format!("{} admits only '{seat}', but {table}'s {effect} rule `{}` requires the written {} to be this caller; writing another principal's value refuses E_RULE",program.name,rule.quarry(),others.join(", ")),
+                    format!("if this trusted seat should write other principals' rows, amend `{effect} <- {}` to `{effect} <- ({}) or viewer = '{seat}'`; the mutation require alone grants no table authority (guide deploy, first admin)",rule.quarry(),rule.quarry())).guide("rules"));
+            });
+        }
+        match statement {
+            Stmt::If { condition,then_body,else_body,.. }=> { body(catalog,schema,program,then_body,seat(condition).map(String::from).or_else(||gate.clone()),out); body(catalog,schema,program,else_body,gate.clone(),out); }
+            Stmt::For { body:inner,.. }=>body(catalog,schema,program,inner,gate.clone(),out),
+            Stmt::As { body:inner,.. }=>body(catalog,schema,program,inner,None,out),
+            Stmt::Return { .. }|Stmt::Break { .. }|Stmt::Continue { .. }=>break,
+            _=>{}
+        }
+    }
+}

## 7. Wave 11 diagnostic precision (QDIAG)

The nullable-reference lint counts a null value as needing no target lookup.
At `if arg != null`, only the null edge carries that exemption; a lookup on
the other edge must still dominate the write. Join by intersection and clear
facts on rebinding. This changes advisory output only.

Operation parameter defaults remain explicit: declare an optional parameter
and bind `value = arg ?? literal`. Refuse `= literal` with that exact idiom.
A multi-line `require` keeps `else CODE` on its closing line; the refusal names
that line and preserves the author's code. `?` precedes `on delete keep`.

Program absence checks on a row lookup (`t[key] = null`) use the unreadable
probe note too. Raw table-rule probes, including delete rules, still observe
all rows; the note's repair states that distinction. A later owner `require`
after `! else NOT_FOUND` gets a refusal-order note only when every read-rule
branch requires that same owner. A branch admitting staff or another party
can legitimately reach the custom refusal and keeps the note quiet.

A child-owner proof requires equality in every child insert branch, immutable
(or unupdatable) child owner/reference and parent owner, no maintained or
field-owned value, no opaque/job writer, no parent expiry (expiry fires no reference action), and no kept parent identity
that ordinary or cascading deletion could recreate,
and equal read rules after replacing
only that owner. No other row fields may appear in either rule. A successful
parent lookup (or a direct probe in its returned row projection) supplies the
parent-readable fact. This is advisory analysis of ordinary admitted writes;
trusted system seeds must preserve the declared application invariant. It never
changes read authority, admission or completeness.

## 8. Wave 11 contextual spellings and refusal codes (QDIAG amendment)

A required same-table row in a reference-typed column, point key, or operation
argument lowers to its `.id`. This includes an inserted snapshot and a looked-up
snapshot; it evaluates its expression once in source order, introduces no
lookup, and grants no existence or read authority. Shorthand fields get the same
projection. Cross-table rows and optional rows remain refused; an optional
upsert must be narrowed with `! else CODE` first. A generic `id` or `principal`
position does not implicitly project a row. Helper parameters retain their
existing call-site row specialization (LLP 3000.007 §5.2; the existing helper implementation specializes same-table rows at the call site): a helper called with
a row keeps its row fields, and one called with a reference must look it up.
Lists and objects receive no deep automatic projection. Explicit `.id` remains
valid. Generated API and runtime IR remain those of the explicit spelling.

Image and video bounds accept integer decimal byte units KB, MB and GB; 5MB
means 5,000,000 bytes. Overflow above u32 refuses at parsing. This is a constant
spelling, not a new value type, upload format, meter or protocol capability.

Readable secondary unique collisions with no authored `else CODE` now return
E_UNIQUE and name the declared key columns, never an internal planner index.
Authored codes still win. The intrinsic id conflict remains E_CONSTRAINT.
Hidden collisions retain E_RULE (and hidden write targets retain NOT_FOUND),
including their deliberately ambiguous public message: a rule denial, unreadable
key holder or physical authorization cut remain indistinguishable. The wording
says that a row you cannot read holds this key as one possible cause. Only the
developer-owned trace may identify the actual private clause. No new hidden
existence bit is exposed; this preserves LLP 3000 §7 and this RFC §5.

The engine/model/oracles preserve rollback, conflict order, null-key uniqueness,
if-absent behavior, device prediction and unchanged named refusal precedence.
Lean's visible secondary conflict code follows E_UNIQUE; explicit row projection
has the existing field semantics. Generated equivalent implicit/explicit sources,
Lean examples, simulator and both device stores exercise the projection and
unique conflict. Native and Lean fixed-seed runs must add no failure signatures.

A development-only check rejects an operation principal argument equal to a
bare declared persona (alice), naming dev:alice. Static principal column literals
get advisory N_PERSONA_PRINCIPAL. Production HTTP does not perform the dev argument check: arbitrary account
principals remain valid and personas confer no authority. The static note is
a compilation advisory whenever `use personas` declares the matching name,
including a production schema that retains its development cast. Developer query/test
output may count observed physical rows filtered by their read rules through
an opt-in collector, outside the wire envelope and durable receipts. It never
adds hidden counts to serve, browser responses, sync, the device or output data;
counts describe encountered rows, not the whole unreadable population.

Every service-seat E_RULE path, durable or ephemeral, for an ordinary table denial, hidden collision
or authorization exhaustion appends the same static authored table rule clause.
It never identifies which cause occurred, or copies row values. This fulfils
the seat explanation without relaxing hidden-collision ambiguity.

The independent Lean surface emitter projects known row-valued columns and
keys from its own source types. Unresolved reference expression types and row
helper specialization outside its fragment are named unsupported cases, never
accepted oracle cases: reference-expression-type and row-helper-snapshot.
The native/compiler and two-device witnesses still exercise row helper fields.
## 12. Wave 11 cap recipe (QDIAG, 2026-10-14)

The resource law and runtime prices above are unchanged. `check` now computes
one recipe for the whole delete across its children, using the ordinary action
reservation plus the root write. Trial caps are evaluated together against all
seven ceilings, including rows and heap, rather than proposing 500 per child
and revealing the summed refusal later. The recipe reports rows/heap and names
each cap to apply. A maintained count source commonly reserves three writes
per child (the row and its old/new aggregate groups). Fixed transient and
retained-event heap matter as well. Existing smaller caps/unique bounds stay
smaller; a cap is always a product decision. Work earlier in the mutation uses
the same meter, so this is a reservation recipe, not a claim for arbitrary
preceding work. If no fixed fitting recipe exists, restrict and a job are the
repair. A maintained-sum source remains excluded from cascades; ordinary
system-job deletes unwind each contribution before the marked parent is deleted.

//! Advisory proof of a child owner copied and checked against a stable parent.
use super::catalog::Catalog;
use crate::ast::{self, Expr, ReadExpr, ShapeField};
use snapback4_core::schema::{Schema, Table, Constraint, ColumnType, RuleExpr, RuleTerm};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;
fn stable(table: &Table, fields: &[&str]) -> bool {
    if fields.iter().any(|field|table.written_by.contains_key(*field)||table.maintained.contains_key(*field)) { return false; }
    matches!(table.rules.update, RuleExpr::Deny) || fields.iter().all(|field| table.constraints.iter().any(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Immutable { fields } if fields.iter().any(|f| f == field))))
}
fn conjuncts(rule: &RuleExpr) -> Vec<&RuleExpr> { match rule { RuleExpr::And(rs) => rs.iter().flat_map(conjuncts).collect(), r => vec![r] } }
fn equality(rule: &RuleExpr, a: &RuleTerm, b: &RuleTerm) -> bool {
    conjuncts(rule).iter().any(|r| matches!(r, RuleExpr::Eq(x,y) if x == a && y == b || x == b && y == a))
}
fn rename(rule: &mut RuleExpr, child: &str, parent: &str) {
    let term = |t: &mut RuleTerm| if matches!(t, RuleTerm::Field(f) if f == child) { *t = RuleTerm::Field(parent.into()); };
    match rule {
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b) | RuleExpr::Ne(a,b) | RuleExpr::Compare(_,a,b) => { term(a); term(b); }
        RuleExpr::And(rs) | RuleExpr::Or(rs) => for r in rs { rename(r,child,parent); },
        RuleExpr::Not(r) => rename(r,child,parent),
        RuleExpr::Exists { key,equalities,.. } | RuleExpr::Created { key,equalities,.. } => { for k in key { term(k); } for (_,v) in equalities { term(v); } },
        _ => {}
    }
}
// @ref LLP 3000.036.002#7 — no application assertion alone verifies stored child ownership.
pub(super) fn readable(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &Schema, read: &ReadExpr, known: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
    let Some(child) = schema.table(&read.table) else { return false; };
    if schema.maintain_for(&read.table).is_some() || system_writes(catalog,&read.table) { return false; }
    for part in &read.keys {
        let Some(reference) = &part.column else { continue; };
        let Some(ColumnType::Ref { table: parent_name }) = child.columns.get(reference) else { continue; };
        let Some(parent) = schema.table(parent_name) else { continue; };
        // Expiry fires no delete action; every expiring parent can lose its identity.
        if parent.expire.is_some() || parent.ephemeral.is_some() { continue; }
        // Immutable owner fields do not prevent a kept id being deleted and recreated.
        if child.on_delete.get(reference).is_none_or(|a| *a==snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Keep)
            && (!matches!(parent.rules.delete,RuleExpr::Deny)
                || parent.on_delete.values().any(|a| *a==snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Cascade)) { continue; }
        if schema.maintain_for(parent_name).is_some() || system_writes(catalog,parent_name) { continue; }
        let input = crate::print::expression(&part.value);
        let known_parent = known.contains(&format!("exists {parent_name}[id = {input}]"));
        let projected_parent = matches!(&part.value, Expr::Member { base,name,.. } if name == "id" && matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal { .. }))
            && projected_from(catalog, read, parent_name);
        if !known_parent && !projected_parent { continue; }
        for (owner, kind) in &child.columns {
            if *kind != ColumnType::Principal || !stable(child, &[reference,owner]) { continue; }
            for (parent_owner, kind) in &parent.columns {
                if *kind != ColumnType::Principal || !stable(parent, &[parent_owner]) { continue; }
                let a = RuleTerm::Field(owner.clone());
                let b = RuleTerm::Reference { table: parent_name.clone(), column: reference.clone(), field: parent_owner.clone(), old:false };
                let verified = child.rules.insert.disjuncts().iter().all(|branch| equality(branch,&a,&b)
                    || equality(branch,&a,&RuleTerm::Viewer) && equality(branch,&b,&RuleTerm::Viewer));
                if !verified || matches!(child.rules.insert,RuleExpr::Deny) { continue; }
                if child.rules.read.any_term(&|t| matches!(t,RuleTerm::Field(f) if f!=owner)) || parent.rules.read.any_term(&|t| matches!(t,RuleTerm::Field(f) if f!=parent_owner)) { continue; }
                let mut readers = child.rules.read.clone(); rename(&mut readers,owner,parent_owner);
                if readers == parent.rules.read { return true; }
            }
        }
    }
    false
}
fn system_writes(catalog:&Catalog,table:&str)->bool {
    catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.programs).filter(|p|p.kind==ast::ProgramKind::Job).any(|p| {
        if p.native { return true; }
        let mut changed=false;let mut body=p.prelude.clone();body.extend(p.body.clone());
        super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body,&mut |e| if matches!(e,Expr::Write { table:t,.. } if t==table) || matches!(e,Expr::Call { .. }) { changed=true; });
        changed
    })
}
fn projected_from(catalog: &Catalog, wanted: &ReadExpr, parent: &str) -> bool {
    let mut found = false;
    for doc in &catalog.documents {
        for p in &doc.programs {
            let mut body = p.body.clone();
            super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body, &mut |expr| {
                if let Expr::Shape { base,fields,.. } = expr && matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Read(r) if r.table == parent) {
                    // Only the direct field's probe has this focal row. Nested shapes do not.
                    found |= fields.iter().any(|f| matches!(f,ShapeField::Field { value:Expr::Exists { read,.. },.. } if read.site == wanted.site));
                }
            });
        }
    }
    found
}
/// Positional typed table arguments denote id, even beside other unique keys.
pub(super) fn typed_id(catalog: &Catalog, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
    let [part] = read.keys.as_slice() else { return false; };
    let Expr::Name { name,.. } = &part.value else { return false; };
    for doc in &catalog.documents {
        for p in &doc.programs {
            if !p.args.iter().any(|a| a.name == *name && matches!(&a.type_.kind,ast::TypeKind::Ref { table } if table == &read.table)) { continue; }
            let mut seen = false; let mut body=p.body.clone();
            super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body,&mut |e| if matches!(e,Expr::Read(r) if r.site == read.site) { seen=true; });
            if seen { return true; }
        }
    }
    false
}
//! Shared type-directed positional point selection for programs and rules.
// @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — identity wins; otherwise ordered compatible declared keys only.
use super::catalog::{Catalog, TableInfo};
use crate::{ast::{IndexDecl, ReadExpr}, diag::Diagnostic, types::Ty};

pub(super) fn candidates<'a>(info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, types: &[(Ty, bool)], synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>) -> Vec<&'a IndexDecl> {
    let identity = read.index.is_none() && types.len() == 1 &&
        (types[0].0.required() == Ty::Id || types[0].0.required() == Ty::Ref(read.table.clone())
            || (!types[0].0.is_optional() && matches!(types[0].0.required(),Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==read.table)));
    info.indexes.iter().filter(|index| !synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())))
        .filter(|index| match &read.index {
            Some(name) => &index.name == name,
            None if identity => index.name == "byId",
            None => index.unique && index.columns.len() == types.len(),
        })
        // Explicit indexes keep their existing mismatch diagnostics and range semantics.
        .filter(|index| read.index.is_some() || types.iter().zip(&index.columns).all(|((ty, literal), column)| {
            let expected = if index.computed.contains_key(column) { Ty::Int }
                else if column == "id" { Ty::Ref(read.table.clone()) }
                else { info.columns.get(column).map(Ty::from_scalar).unwrap_or(Ty::Unknown) };
            ty.comparable(&expected) || (!ty.is_optional() && matches!((ty.required(),expected.required()),(Ty::Row(a)|Ty::WriteRow(a),Ty::Ref(b)) if a==b)) || (*literal && expected.is_identity())
        })).collect()
}

pub(super) fn refusal(info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, candidates: &[&IndexDecl], catalog: &Catalog) -> Diagnostic {
    let alternatives = if candidates.is_empty() && read.index.is_some() {
        info.indexes.iter().filter(|index| !catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>()
    } else { candidates.to_vec() };
    // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.7 — `budgets['general']` (lane 020): a text value names the declared
    // text key far more often than a row id, so that named form leads (and is --fix's choice).
    let mut alternatives = alternatives;
    alternatives.sort_by_key(|index| index.columns.iter().any(|column| column == "id"));
    let mut changes = Vec::new();
    for index in &alternatives {
        // Same-column opposite-order indexes need the explicit name as well.
        let duplicate = info.indexes.iter().any(|other| other.name != index.name && other.unique
            && other.columns.len() == index.columns.len() && other.columns.iter().all(|c| index.columns.contains(c)));
        if read.index.is_none() { changes.push(crate::repair::ReadChange::NamedKey { columns: index.columns.clone(), index: duplicate.then(|| index.name.clone()) }); }
        // Unknown explicit indexes and computed keys retain verified legacy alternatives.
        changes.push(crate::repair::ReadChange::Index(index.name.clone()));
    }
    let message = if alternatives.is_empty() {
        format!("{} has no declared unique key accepting these positional argument types in order; name the key columns, or use where for a population", read.table)
    } else {
        format!("{} positional argument types leave {} candidate keys; name the intended key columns or choose a compatible unique index explicitly", read.table, candidates.len())
    };
    let diagnostic = Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN", message, "").guide("reads").repair_read(&read.site, changes);
    // The user explicitly opts into one context-verified choice with --fix.
    if read.index.is_none() && candidates.len() > 1 { diagnostic.mechanical() } else { diagnostic }
}
//! Bind key operands once, retaining authored sequencing and contextual literals.
use super::*;

impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
    pub(super) fn bind_key_parts(&mut self, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, end: usize,
        keys: &mut Vec<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<()> {
        for (position, part) in read.keys.iter().enumerate().take(end).skip(keys.len()) {
            let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
            let expected = part.column.as_deref().or_else(|| read.index.as_ref().and_then(|name| info.indexes.iter().find(|i| &i.name == name)).and_then(|i| i.columns.get(position)).map(String::as_str)).and_then(|column| key_identity(info, column));
            let (e, t) = match expected {
                Some(expected) => self.expr_as_reference(&part.value, &mut setup, &expected)?,
                None => self.expr(&part.value, &mut setup)?,
            };
            self.sequence_setup(keys.iter_mut().map(|(_, e, _)| e), setup, out);
            let e = if expr::allocates(&e) { self.snapshot(e, out) } else { e };
            keys.push((part.column.clone(), e, t));
        }
        if let Some(index) = read.index.as_ref().and_then(|name| info.indexes.iter().find(|i| &i.name == name)) {
            for (position,(column,value,ty)) in keys.iter_mut().enumerate() {
                if let Some(expected) = column.as_deref().or_else(|| index.columns.get(position).map(String::as_str)).and_then(|c| key_identity(info,c)) {
                    (*value,*ty) = self.row_identity(value.clone(),ty.clone(),&expected);
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    // Mixed-key normalization names an already-bound positional prefix. Only
    // a literal acquires new context; compound expressions keep their IR/setup.
    pub(super) fn name_bound_keys(&mut self, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr,
        keys: &mut [(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)]) -> Fallible<()> {
        for ((column, expr, ty), part) in keys.iter_mut().zip(&read.keys) {
            *column = part.column.clone();
            if let Some(expected) = column.as_deref().and_then(|column| key_identity(info, column))
                && matches!(&part.value, A::Literal { value: Literal::Str(_), .. }) {
                (*expr, *ty) = self.expr_as_reference(&part.value, &mut setup::Body::default(), &expected)?;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

fn key_identity(info: &TableInfo, column: &str) -> Option<Ty> {
    let ty = if column == "id" { Ty::Ref(info.decl.name.clone()) }
        else { Ty::from_scalar(info.columns.get(column)?) };
    ty.required().is_identity().then_some(ty)
}

diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/persona_principals.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/persona_principals.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..d7f37c0772
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/persona_principals.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,33 @@
+//! Development argument checks shared by the host and fixture runner.
+use serde_json::Value as Json;
+#[cfg(test)] use serde_json::json;
+use snapback4_core::Refusal;
+use std::collections::BTreeMap;
+pub(crate) fn arguments(personas:&[String],kinds:&BTreeMap<String,snapback4_core::ir::ArgType>,args:&Json)->Option<Refusal> {
+    fn bare(kind:&snapback4_core::ir::ArgType,value:&Json,personas:&[String])->Option<String> {
+        use snapback4_core::ir::ArgType;
+        match kind {
+            ArgType::Principal => value.as_str().filter(|v| personas.iter().any(|p| p==v)).map(String::from),
+            ArgType::Optional(inner)=>bare(inner,value,personas),
+            ArgType::List { of:Some(inner),.. }|ArgType::ListRange { of:Some(inner),.. }=>value.as_array()?.iter().find_map(|v|bare(inner,v,personas)),
+            _=>None
+        }
+    }
+    let fields=args.as_object()?;
+    kinds.iter().find_map(|(name,kind)|bare(kind,fields.get(name)?,personas).map(|p|Refusal::new("E_INPUT","input",format!("argument {name}: '{p}' is a persona name, not a principal; write 'dev:{p}' (or use the authenticated account principal in production)")).guide("personas")))
+}
+#[cfg(test)]
+mod qdiag_tests {
+ use super::*;
+ use snapback4_core::ir::ArgType;
+ #[test] fn declared_persona_principals_are_checked_without_rewriting_identity(){
+  let personas=vec!["alice".into()];let kinds=BTreeMap::from([("p".into(),ArgType::Principal)]);
+  let value=json!({"p":"alice"});
+  let why=arguments(&personas,&kinds,&value).unwrap();assert_eq!(why.code,"E_INPUT");assert!(why.message.contains("dev:alice"));assert_eq!(value,json!({"p":"alice"}));
+  for p in ["dev:alice","aliceOther","user:alice","service:alice"] {assert!(arguments(&personas,&kinds,&json!({"p":p})).is_none());}
+  assert!(arguments(&[],&kinds,&value).is_none());
+  assert!(arguments(&personas,&BTreeMap::from([("p".into(),ArgType::Id)]),&value).is_none());
+  let lists=BTreeMap::from([("p".into(),ArgType::Optional(Box::new(ArgType::List {of:Some(Box::new(ArgType::Principal)),max:5})))]);
+  assert!(arguments(&personas,&lists,&json!({"p":["dev:alice","alice"]})).is_some());
+ }
+}

                 for (name, value) in fields {

+    if !shared.personas { return None; }
+    let bound = lock(&shared.bound);
+    crate::persona_principals::arguments(&bound.personas,kinds,args)

+pub fn seat_refusal(schema:&Schema,error:&RuntimeError,viewer:&str)->Refusal {

//! A fitting recipe for the whole delete, evaluated with the engine's prices.
use snapback4_core::schema::{Schema, Constraint, references::{OnDelete, Price}};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

pub(super) fn reaches(schema:&Schema,root:&str,target:&str)->bool {
    let mut seen=BTreeSet::from([root.to_owned()]);let mut todo=vec![root.to_owned()];
    while let Some(parent)=todo.pop() {
        if parent==target {return true;}
        for r in schema.referencing(&parent) {if r.action==OnDelete::Cascade && seen.insert(r.child.into()) {todo.push(r.child.into());}}
    }
    false
}

// @ref LLP 3000.039#7 — caps share one delete reservation, never a per-child allowance.
pub(super) fn recipe(schema: &Schema, parent: &str, job: bool) -> String {
    let mut targets = BTreeSet::new();
    let mut reached = BTreeSet::from([parent.to_owned()]);
    let mut todo = vec![parent.to_owned()];
    while let Some(table) = todo.pop() {
        for r in schema.referencing(&table) {
            if r.action.writes() { targets.insert((r.child.to_owned(), r.column.to_owned())); }
            if r.action == OnDelete::Cascade && reached.insert(r.child.to_owned()) { todo.push(r.child.to_owned()); }
        }
    }
    let candidate = |cap: u32| {
        let mut trial = schema.clone();
        for (child, column) in &targets {
            // A unique reference already has the tightest possible bound.
            if trial.reference_cap(child, column) == Some(1) { continue; }
            let table = trial.tables.get_mut(child).unwrap();
            let mut found = false;
            for constraint in &mut table.constraints {
                if let Constraint::Cap { by, max } = constraint && by.len() == 1 && &by[0] == column {
                    *max = (*max).min(cap); found = true;
                }
            }
            if !found { table.constraints.push(Constraint::Cap { by: vec![column.clone()], max: cap }); }
        }
        trial.prepare_write_reservations();
        let price = trial.delete_total_reservation(parent)?;
        Some((trial, price))
    };
    let fits = |price: &Price| super::reference_actions::over_ceiling(price, job).is_none();
    let fallback = format!("keep `on delete restrict` and delete the child rows first in a job (`for row in <child>:`), then delete the {parent} row; choose caps only if they are product limits (guide writes)");
    if !candidate(1).is_some_and(|(_, price)| fits(&price)) { return format!("even cap 1 cannot fit this delete's fixed heap/row reservation; {fallback}"); }
    let (mut low, mut high) = (1, 500);
    while low < high {
        let mid = low + (high - low + 1) / 2;
        if candidate(mid).is_some_and(|(_, price)| fits(&price)) { low = mid; } else { high = mid - 1; }
    }
    let (trial, price) = candidate(low).unwrap();
    let unique=targets.iter().map(|(child,column)|format!("`unique {column}` on {child}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" or ");
    let caps = targets.iter().map(|(child, column)| format!("`cap {} by {column}` on {child}", trial.reference_cap(child, column).unwrap())).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
    let limits = if job { snapback4_core::Ceilings::job() } else { snapback4_core::Ceilings::default() };
    format!("apply {caps} together: the whole delete (including the root) reserves {} / {} rows written and {} / {} heap bytes, with all indexes, derived writes and event images priced together. A count-maintained source commonly reserves 3 × its rows (the row plus its old and new aggregate groups), not one row per child; for a one-to-one relation use {unique} instead; {fallback}", price[3], limits.rows_written, price[4], limits.heap_bytes)
}
//! Bind key operands once, retaining authored sequencing and contextual literals.
use super::*;

impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
    pub(super) fn bind_key_parts(&mut self, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, end: usize,
        keys: &mut Vec<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<()> {
        for (position, part) in read.keys.iter().enumerate().take(end).skip(keys.len()) {
            let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
            let expected = part.column.as_deref().or_else(|| read.index.as_ref().and_then(|name| info.indexes.iter().find(|i| &i.name == name)).and_then(|i| i.columns.get(position)).map(String::as_str)).and_then(|column| key_identity(info, column));
            let (e, t) = match expected {
                Some(expected) => self.expr_as_reference(&part.value, &mut setup, &expected)?,
                None => self.expr(&part.value, &mut setup)?,
            };
            self.sequence_setup(keys.iter_mut().map(|(_, e, _)| e), setup, out);
            let e = if expr::allocates(&e) { self.snapshot(e, out) } else { e };
            keys.push((part.column.clone(), e, t));
        }
        if let Some(index) = read.index.as_ref().and_then(|name| info.indexes.iter().find(|i| &i.name == name)) {
            for (position,(column,value,ty)) in keys.iter_mut().enumerate() {
                if let Some(expected) = column.as_deref().or_else(|| index.columns.get(position).map(String::as_str)).and_then(|c| key_identity(info,c)) {
                    (*value,*ty) = self.row_identity(value.clone(),ty.clone(),&expected);
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    // Mixed-key normalization names an already-bound positional prefix. Only
    // a literal acquires new context; compound expressions keep their IR/setup.
    pub(super) fn name_bound_keys(&mut self, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr,
        keys: &mut [(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)]) -> Fallible<()> {
        for ((column, expr, ty), part) in keys.iter_mut().zip(&read.keys) {
            *column = part.column.clone();
            if let Some(expected) = column.as_deref().and_then(|column| key_identity(info, column))
                && matches!(&part.value, A::Literal { value: Literal::Str(_), .. }) {
                (*expr, *ty) = self.expr_as_reference(&part.value, &mut setup::Body::default(), &expected)?;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

fn key_identity(info: &TableInfo, column: &str) -> Option<Ty> {
    let ty = if column == "id" { Ty::Ref(info.decl.name.clone()) }
        else { Ty::from_scalar(info.columns.get(column)?) };
    ty.required().is_identity().then_some(ty)
}

use snapback4_lang::compile;
fn checked(source: &str) -> snapback4_lang::Bound { compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]) }
const REFS: &str = "table parents:\n  read <- allow\ntable kids:\n  parent: parents ? on delete keep\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n";
#[test]
fn optional_reference_checks_join_the_null_edge() {
    for condition in ["parent != null", "not (parent = null)", "null != parent"] {
        let b = checked(&format!("{REFS}mutation add(parent: parents ?):\n  if {condition}:\n    row = parents[parent] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  insert kids {{ parent }}\n"));
        assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
        assert!(!b.notes.iter().any(|n| n.code == "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED"), "{:?}", b.notes);
    }
    for body in ["  if parent != null:\n    unused = true\n", "  if parent != null and skip:\n    row = parents[parent] ! else NOT_FOUND\n"] {
        let b = checked(&format!("{REFS}mutation add(parent: parents ?, other: parents ?, skip: bool):\n{body}  insert kids {{ parent }}\n"));
        assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
        assert!(b.notes.iter().any(|n| n.code == "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED"), "{:?}", b.notes);
    }
}
#[test]
fn syntax_repairs_name_the_actual_form() {
    let b = checked("mutation add(body: text <=20 = 'hi'):\n  return body\n");
    assert!(b.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.rewrite.contains("body ?? 'hi'") && d.rewrite.contains("text <=20 ?")), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
    let b = checked("mutation add(a: bool, b: bool):\n  require (a or\n    b)\n  else NOPE\n  return true\n");
    assert!(b.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.rewrite.contains("closing line") && d.rewrite.contains("else NOPE")), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
    let b = checked("table parents:\n  public\ntable kids:\n  parent: parents on delete keep ?\n");
    assert!(b.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.rewrite == "parent: parents ? on delete keep"), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
}

#[test]
fn whole_cascade_recipe_fits_once_across_children() {
    let source="table roots:\n  owner: principal\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  delete <- .owner = viewer\n  sync\ntable votes:\n  root: roots on delete cascade\n  owner: principal\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  sync\ntable comments:\n  root: roots on delete cascade\n  owner: principal\n  body: text <=10000\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  sync\nmutation remove(root: roots):\n  delete roots[root]\n";
    for source in [source.to_owned(),source.replace("  owner: principal\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  sync\ntable comments:","  owner: principal\n  cap 500 by root\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  sync\ntable comments:").replace("  body: text <=10000\n","  body: text <=10000\n  cap 500 by root\n")] {
        let b=checked(&source);
        let d=b.diagnostics.iter().find(|d|d.code=="E_CASCADE_BOUND").unwrap_or_else(||panic!("{:?}",b.diagnostics));
        assert!(d.rewrite.contains("heap bytes") && d.rewrite.contains("3 ×"),"{}",d.rewrite);
        let caps: Vec<_>=d.rewrite.split('`').filter(|s|s.starts_with("cap ")).collect();
        assert_eq!(caps.len(),2,"{}",d.rewrite);
        let edited=source.lines().filter(|line|!line.trim().starts_with("cap 500")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
        let edited=edited.replace("  root: roots on delete cascade",&format!("  root: roots on delete cascade\n  {}",caps[0]));
        let fixed=checked(&(edited+"\n"));
        assert!(fixed.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{}\n{:?}",d.rewrite,fixed.diagnostics);
        let price=fixed.schema.delete_total_reservation("roots").unwrap();
        let ceiling=snapback4_core::Ceilings::default();
        assert!(price[3]<=ceiling.rows_written && price[4]<=ceiling.heap_bytes,"{price:?}");
    }
}
const COERCION: &str="table parents:\n  name: text <=20\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\ntable kids:\n  parent: parents on delete keep\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\nfn label(p: parents): p.name\nquery parentName(p: parents):\n  return parents[p] { name }\nmutation add(id: id):\n  p = insert parents { id, name: 'new' }\n  insert kids { id: 'child', parent: p }\n  return { label: label(p), key: parents[p] }\n";
#[test]
fn contextual_row_ids_preserve_helper_snapshots_and_explicit_ir() {
    let implicit=checked(COERCION);
    assert!(implicit.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",implicit.diagnostics);
    let explicit=checked(&COERCION.replace("parent: p }","parent: p.id }").replace("key: parents[p]","key: parents[p.id]"));
    assert!(explicit.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",explicit.diagnostics);
    fn erase_sites(v:&mut serde_json::Value) { match v { serde_json::Value::Object(o)=> { o.remove("site"); for v in o.values_mut(){erase_sites(v);} }, serde_json::Value::Array(a)=>for v in a{erase_sites(v);},_=>{} } }
    let programs=|b:&snapback4_lang::Bound| {let mut v=serde_json::to_value(b.programs.iter().map(|p|&p.program).collect::<Vec<_>>()).unwrap();erase_sites(&mut v);v};
    assert_eq!(programs(&implicit),programs(&explicit));
    for source in [COERCION.replace("parent: p }","parent: (parents[id]) }"),COERCION.replace("id: 'child', parent: p","id: p, parent: p"),COERCION.replace("table kids:","table strangers:\n  public\ntable kids:").replace("parent: parents on delete keep","parent: strangers on delete keep")] {
        let refused=checked(&source); assert!(!refused.diagnostics.is_empty(),"unexpectedly accepted: {source}");
    }
}
#[test]
fn media_units_and_precise_type_repairs() {
    let b=checked("table pictures:\n  photo: image <=5MB\n  video: video <=2GB ?\n  public\n");
    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);
    assert!(checked("table pictures:\n  photo: image <=5GB\n").diagnostics.iter().any(|d|d.message.contains("4,294,967,295")));
    assert!(checked("table pictures:\n  photo: image\n").diagnostics.iter().any(|d|d.rewrite.contains("image <=5MB")));
    let b=checked("table values:\n  owner: principal\n  n: int 1..100 ?\n  public\nview totals: sum values of .n by .owner\n");
    assert!(b.diagnostics.iter().any(|d|d.message.contains("optional") && d.message.contains("range is accepted")),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);
    let b=checked("table names:\n  name: text <=10\n  public\nmutation add(name: text <=10):\n  insert names { name: lower(name) }\n");
    assert!(b.diagnostics.iter().any(|d|d.rewrite.contains("Unicode") && d.rewrite.contains("3 ×")),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);
    let b=checked("query q(text: text <=20, q: text <=10):\n  return text matching q first 5 by .id\n");
    assert!(b.diagnostics.iter().any(|d|d.rewrite.contains("tokens(text)")),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);
}
#[test]
fn seats_and_hidden_refusal_order_have_actionable_notes() {
    let b=checked("use personas alice\ntable rows:\n  owner: principal\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\nmutation create(owner: principal):\n  require viewer = 'service:ops' else NOPE\n  insert rows { owner }\nmutation access(row: rows):\n  r = rows[row] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  require r.owner = viewer else NOT_OWNER\n  return r\nmutation bad():\n  insert rows { owner: 'alice' }\n");
    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);
    for (code,word) in [("N_WRITE_DENIED","or viewer = 'service:ops'"),("N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN","never NOT_OWNER"),("N_PERSONA_PRINCIPAL","dev:alice")] {assert!(b.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code==code && (n.message.contains(word)||n.rewrite.contains(word))),"{:?}",b.notes);}
}
#[test]
fn small_population_filter_and_verified_child_ownership_are_quiet(){
 let source="table rows:\n  owner: principal\n  score: int\n  cap 20\n  public\nquery page(c: cursor ?):\n  return rows where .score > 0 first 10 by .id after c\n";
 assert!(!checked(source).notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_SPARSE_FILTER"));
 let source="table parents:\n  owner: principal\n  immutable owner\n  read <- .owner = viewer\ntable kids:\n  parent: parents on delete keep\n  owner: principal\n  unique parent\n  immutable parent,owner\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer and .parent.owner = viewer\nmutation check(parent: parents):\n  p = parents[parent] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  require p.owner = viewer else NOPE\n  require not exists kids[parent = parent] else HAS_CHILD\n  return true\nquery list(c: cursor ?):\n  return parents first 10 by .id after c { id, has: exists kids[parent = .id] }\n";
 let b=checked(source);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);
 assert!(!b.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_UNREADABLE_PROBE" && n.message.contains("kids")),"{:?}",b.notes);
 let unverified=checked(&source.replace("and .parent.owner = viewer",""));
 assert!(unverified.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_UNREADABLE_PROBE" && n.message.contains("kids")));
}

#[test]
fn every_prescribed_syntax_rewrite_compiles() {
 for source in [
  "table media:\n  photo: image <=5MB\n  public\n",
  "table names:\n  name: text <=30\n  public\n  insert <- allow\nmutation add(name: text <=10):\n  insert names {name: lower(name)}\n",
  "fn twice(n: int):\n  return n + n\nquery calc():\n  return twice(2)\n",
  "table events:\n  owner: principal\n  read <- .owner = viewer\nview totals: count events by .owner\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n",
  "mutation add(body: text <=20 ?):\n  bodyValue = body ?? 'hi'\n  return bodyValue\n",
  "mutation add(a: bool,b: bool):\n  require (a or\n    b) else NOPE\n  return true\n",
  "table parents:\n  public\ntable kids:\n  parent: parents ? on delete keep\n",
  "table values:\n  owner: principal\n  n: int 1..100\n  public\nview totals: sum values of .n by .owner\n",
  "query check(text: text <=20,q: text <=10):\n  return q in tokens(text)\n",
 ] { assert!(checked(source).diagnostics.is_empty(),"{source}: {:?}",checked(source).diagnostics); }
}
#[test]
fn maintained_sum_bulk_unwind_is_an_ordinary_job() {
 let source="table parents:\n  public\n  delete <- allow\ntable kids:\n  parent: parents on delete cascade\n  owner: principal\n  n: int\n  cap 20 by parent\n  public\nview totals: sum kids of .n by .owner\n";
 let b=checked(source);assert!(b.diagnostics.iter().any(|d|d.code=="E_REFERENCE_ACTION" && d.rewrite.contains("each delete updates the sum")),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);
 let fixed=source.replace("on delete cascade","on delete restrict")+"job unwind():\n  for row in kids:\n    delete kids[row.id]\n";
 assert!(checked(&fixed).diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",checked(&fixed).diagnostics);
}
#[test]
fn defaults_and_job_literals_use_the_persona_note() {
 let b=checked("use personas alice\ntable profiles:\n  owner: principal default 'alice'\n  public\n  insert <- allow\njob build():\n  for row in profiles:\n    insert profiles { owner:'alice' }\n");
 assert_eq!(b.notes.iter().filter(|n|n.code=="N_PERSONA_PRINCIPAL").count(),2,"{:?}",b.notes);
}
#[test]
fn media_unit_spelling_has_identical_backend_bounds_and_model_exclusion(){
 let units="table media:\n  photo: image <=5MB\n  clip: video <=2GB ?\n  public\n";
 let bytes=units.replace("5MB","5000000").replace("2GB","2000000000");
 let a=checked(units);let b=checked(&bytes);assert!(a.diagnostics.is_empty() && b.diagnostics.is_empty());
 assert_eq!(a.schema,b.schema);
 let model=|source:&str|format!("{:?}",snapback4_lang::emit::lean::lean(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]).unwrap_err());
 assert_eq!(model(units),model(&bytes));
}
#[test]
fn nested_cascade_has_one_consistent_recipe_for_the_authored_root(){
 let source="table roots:\n  public\n  delete <- allow\ntable children:\n  root: roots on delete cascade\n  public\ntable leaves:\n  child: children on delete cascade\n  public\nmutation remove(root: roots):\n  delete roots[root]\n";
 let b=checked(source);let ds=b.diagnostics.iter().filter(|d|d.code=="E_CASCADE_BOUND").collect::<Vec<_>>();assert_eq!(ds.len(),1,"{:?}",b.diagnostics);
 let caps=ds[0].rewrite.split('`').filter(|s|s.starts_with("cap ")).collect::<Vec<_>>();assert_eq!(caps.len(),2);
 let fixed=source.replace("  root: roots on delete cascade",&format!("  root: roots on delete cascade\n  {}",caps.iter().find(|c|c.ends_with("by root")).unwrap())).replace("  child: children on delete cascade",&format!("  child: children on delete cascade\n  {}",caps.iter().find(|c|c.ends_with("by child")).unwrap()));
 assert!(checked(&fixed).diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",checked(&fixed).diagnostics);
}
#[test]
fn owner_equality_does_not_survive_kept_parent_recreation_or_expiry(){
 let base="table parents:\n  owner: principal\n  immutable owner\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\ntable kids:\n  parent: parents on delete keep\n  owner: principal\n  unique parent\n  immutable parent,owner\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer and .parent.owner = viewer\nmutation probe(parent: parents):\n  p = parents[parent] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  require not exists kids[parent = parent] else HAS_CHILD\n  return true\n";
 assert!(!checked(base).notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_UNREADABLE_PROBE"));
 for source in [base.replace("  insert <- .owner = viewer\ntable kids:","  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  delete <- .owner = viewer\ntable kids:"),base.replace("  immutable owner\n","  immutable owner\n  until: time\n  by byUntil: until\n  expire at .until\n"),base.replace("table parents:\n","table roots:\n  public\n  delete <- allow\ntable parents:\n  root: roots on delete cascade\n  cap 1 by root\n")] {
  let b=checked(&source);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);assert!(b.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_UNREADABLE_PROBE"),"{:?}",b.notes);
 }
 let expires=base.replace("  immutable owner\n","  immutable owner\n  until: time\n  by byUntil: until\n  expire at .until\n");
 for action in ["on delete restrict","on delete cascade",""] {let b=checked(&expires.replace("on delete keep",action));assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{action}: {:?}",b.diagnostics);assert!(b.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_UNREADABLE_PROBE"),"{action}: {:?}",b.notes);}

}
#[test]
fn nested_read_alternatives_can_reach_the_custom_require(){
 let source="table rows:\n  owner: principal\n  read <- (.owner = viewer or viewer = 'service:ops') and viewer != 'blocked'\nmutation access(row: rows):\n  r = rows[row] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  require r.owner = viewer else NOT_OWNER\n  return r\n";
 let b=checked(source);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);assert!(!b.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN"),"{:?}",b.notes);
 let owner=checked(&source.replace("(.owner = viewer or viewer = 'service:ops')", "(.owner = viewer or .owner = viewer)"));assert!(owner.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN"));
}
#[test]
fn required_row_expression_keys_keep_intrinsic_identity_precedence(){
 let source="table parents:\n  sibling: parents ? on delete keep\n  unique sibling\n  public\nquery get(p: parents):\n  return parents[parents[p] ! else NOT_FOUND]\n";
 let b=checked(source);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);
 let explicit=checked(&source.replace("return parents[parents[p] ! else NOT_FOUND]","return parents[(parents[p] ! else NOT_FOUND).id]"));assert!(explicit.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",explicit.diagnostics);
 let strip=|b:&snapback4_lang::Bound|{let mut v=serde_json::to_value(&b.programs[0].program).unwrap();fn erase(v:&mut serde_json::Value){match v{serde_json::Value::Object(o)=>{o.remove("site");for v in o.values_mut(){erase(v);}},serde_json::Value::Array(a)=>for v in a{erase(v);},_=>{}}}erase(&mut v);v};assert_eq!(strip(&b),strip(&explicit));
}

#[test]
fn required_rows_project_into_nullable_reference_keys(){
 let tables="table parents:\n  public\ntable kids:\n  parent: parents ? on delete keep\n  unique byParent: parent\n  public\n";
 for key in ["kids[parent = r]","kids[r] by byParent","kids[parent = r] by byParent"] {
  let source=format!("{tables}query child(parent: parents):\n  r = parents[parent] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  return {key}\n");let b=checked(&source);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{key}: {:?}",b.diagnostics);
  let explicit=source.replace("parent = r]","parent = r.id]").replace("kids[r]","kids[r.id]");assert!(checked(&explicit).diagnostics.is_empty());
  let optional=source.replace("parents[parent] ! else NOT_FOUND","parents[parent]");assert!(!checked(&optional).diagnostics.is_empty(),"optional source row accepted: {key}");
 }
}
use snapback4_core::{Context, Engine, Value, WriteState, engine::refusal_trace::RefusalTrace};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
fn id(s:&str)->Value{Value::Id(s.into())}
fn text(s:&str)->Value{Value::String(s.into())}
fn args(v:&[(&str,Value)])->BTreeMap<String,Value>{v.iter().map(|(k,v)|(k.to_string(),v.clone())).collect()}
fn engine(source:&str)->Engine{let b=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);Engine::memory(b.schema,b.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program)).unwrap()}
fn why(e:&mut Engine,op:&str,who:&str,values:&[(&str,Value)])->snapback4_core::Refusal{match e.mutate(op,None,Context::new(who,args(values)).at(100)).unwrap().state{WriteState::Failed {why,..}=>why,s=>panic!("{s:?}")}}
const UNIQUE:&str="table rows:\n  owner: principal\n  writer: principal\n  handle: text <=20\n  other: text <=20 ?\n  unique handle\n  unique other else OTHER_TAKEN 'Other key is taken'\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .writer = viewer\n  update <- .writer = viewer\nmutation add(id: id, owner: principal, writer: principal, handle: text <=20, other: text <=20 ?):\n  return insert rows {id,owner,writer,handle,other}\nmutation absent(id: id, owner: principal, writer: principal, handle: text <=20, other: text <=20 ?):\n  return insert rows {id,owner,writer,handle,other} if absent\nquery page(c: cursor ?):\n  return rows first 10 by .id after c\n";
fn seed(e:&mut Engine,id_:&str,owner:&str,handle:&str,other:Option<&str>){e.seed(vec![("rows".into(),snapback4_core::value::object([("id",id(id_)),("owner",id(owner)),("writer",id(owner)),("handle",text(handle)),("other",other.map(text).unwrap_or(Value::Null))]))]).unwrap();}
fn input(id_:&str,owner:&str,handle:&str,other:Option<&str>)->Vec<(&'static str,Value)>{vec![("id",id(id_)),("owner",id(owner)),("writer",id(owner)),("handle",text(handle)),("other",other.map(text).unwrap_or(Value::Null))]}
#[test]
fn visible_unique_names_the_declaration_and_authored_code_wins(){
 let mut e=engine(UNIQUE);seed(&mut e,"old","alice","key",Some("other"));
 let visible=why(&mut e,"add","alice",&input("new","alice","key",None));
 assert_eq!(visible.code,"E_UNIQUE");assert!(visible.message.contains("unique handle") && !visible.message.contains("$intent"));
 let named=why(&mut e,"add","alice",&input("new","alice","different",Some("other")));assert_eq!(named.code,"OTHER_TAKEN");
 assert_eq!(why(&mut e,"add","alice",&input("old","alice","different",None)).code,"E_CONSTRAINT");
 let absent=e.mutate("absent",None,Context::new("alice",args(&input("new","alice","key",None))).at(100)).unwrap();
 assert!(matches!(absent.state,WriteState::Sent {result:Value::Null,..}));
 // Null secondary keys do not conflict; the handle remains independently unique.
 for (i,handle) in [("two","two"),("three","three")] {assert!(matches!(e.mutate("add",None,Context::new("alice",args(&input(i,"alice",handle,None))).at(100)).unwrap().state,WriteState::Sent {..}));}
}
#[test]
fn hidden_unique_is_the_same_public_refusal_as_rule_denial_including_seats(){
 for viewer in ["alice","service:ops"] {
  let mut e=engine(UNIQUE);seed(&mut e,"old","bob","key",None);
  let collision=why(&mut e,"add",viewer,&input("new",viewer,"key",None));
  let mut denied=input("new",viewer,"different",None);denied[2].1=id("other");
  let denial=why(&mut e,"add",viewer,&denied);
  assert_eq!(collision,denial,"{viewer}");assert_eq!(collision.code,"E_RULE");assert!(collision.message.contains("a row you cannot read holds this key"));
  if viewer.starts_with("service:"){assert!(collision.message.contains("rows rule clause: insert <- .writer = viewer"));}
  assert_eq!(e.store().rows("rows").unwrap().len(),1);
 }
}
#[test]
fn hidden_counter_is_request_local_and_changes_no_query_envelope_or_meter(){
 let mut e=engine(UNIQUE);seed(&mut e,"a","alice","a",None);seed(&mut e,"b","bob","b",None);
 let plain=e.query("page",Context::new("alice",args(&[("c",Value::Null)])).at(100)).unwrap();
 let trace=RefusalTrace::default();
 let traced=e.query("page",Context::new("alice",args(&[("c",Value::Null)])).at(100).with_refusal_trace(trace.clone())).unwrap();
 assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(plain).unwrap(),serde_json::to_value(traced).unwrap());
 assert_eq!(trace.hidden(),1);
 let next=RefusalTrace::default();e.query("page",Context::new(snapback4_core::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL,args(&[("c",Value::Null)])).at(100).with_refusal_trace(next.clone())).unwrap();assert_eq!(next.hidden(),0);
}
#[test]
fn ephemeral_seat_denials_have_the_same_static_clause(){
 let mut e=engine("table typing:\n  owner: principal\n  unique owner\n  cap 50\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  ephemeral 4s\nmutation add(owner: principal):\n  return insert typing {owner}\n");
 let denied=why(&mut e,"add","service:ops",&[("owner",id("alice"))]);assert_eq!(denied.code,"E_RULE");assert!(denied.message.ends_with("typing rule clause: insert <- .owner = viewer"),"{denied:?}");
}
#[test]
fn hidden_point_images_are_counted_outside_the_query_envelope(){
 let source=format!("{UNIQUE}query point(key: rows):\n  return rows[key]\n");let mut e=engine(&source);seed(&mut e,"hidden","bob","key",None);
 let trace=RefusalTrace::default();let values=args(&[("key",id("hidden"))]);
 let plain=e.query("point",Context::new("alice",values.clone()).at(100)).unwrap();
 let traced=e.query("point",Context::new("alice",values).at(100).with_refusal_trace(trace.clone())).unwrap();assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(plain).unwrap(),serde_json::to_value(traced).unwrap());assert_eq!(trace.hidden(),1);
}

#[test]
fn calendar_unique_conflict_names_the_declared_expression(){
 let mut e=engine("table visits:\n  at: time\n  unique day(at)\n  public\n  insert <- allow\nmutation add(id: id, at: time):\n  return insert visits {id, at}\n");
 assert!(matches!(e.mutate("add",None,Context::new("alice",args(&[("id",id("a")),("at",Value::Int(0))])).at(100)).unwrap().state,WriteState::Sent {..}));
 let conflict=why(&mut e,"add","alice",&[("id",id("b")),("at",Value::Int(1000))]);assert_eq!(conflict.code,"E_UNIQUE");assert!(conflict.message.contains("unique day(at)"),"{conflict:?}");
}

+    pub(super) fn expr_as_reference(&mut self, expr: &A, out: &mut setup::Body, expected: &Ty) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
+        let (value, ty) = self.expr_as_identity(expr, out, expected)?;
+        Ok(self.row_identity(value, ty, expected))
+    }
+
+    // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#8 — contextual projection is exactly `.id`,
+    // without a lookup; optional and cross-table rows still need an edit.
+    pub(super) fn row_identity(&self, value: Expr, ty: Ty, expected: &Ty) -> (Expr, Ty) {
+        if !ty.is_optional() && matches!(ty.required(), Ty::Row(_) | Ty::WriteRow(_)) {
+            let table = match ty.required() { Ty::Row(t) | Ty::WriteRow(t) => t, _ => unreachable!() };
+            if expected.required() == Ty::Ref(table.clone()) {
+                return (ir::field(value, "id"), Ty::Ref(table));
+            }
+        }
+        (value, ty)
+    }
