//! Lifetime-bound rule inputs, distinct from historical recorded attribution.
// @ref LLP 3000.014#4-the-capture — captures have their parent's type and activation lifecycle.
use super::*;

pub const PARENT_DEADLINE: &str = "$parentDeadline";
pub const DEADLINE: &str = "$deadline";
pub const CHILDREN: &str = "$children";

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Through {
    pub reference: String,
    pub parent: String,
    /// Child engine field -> immediate parent field (including its captures).
    pub fields: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    pub identity: String,
    pub expiring: bool,
}

impl Table {
    pub fn engine_field(&self, field: &str) -> bool {
        (self.ephemeral.is_some() && field == DEADLINE) || self.recorded.contains_key(field)
            || self.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| {
                t.fields.contains_key(field) || matches!(field, DEADLINE | PARENT_DEADLINE)
            })
    }
    /// One classification for every expiry gate, including inherited deadlines.
    pub fn is_expiring(&self) -> bool {
        self.ephemeral.is_some() || self.expire.is_some() || self.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| t.expiring)
    }
    pub fn expiry_column(&self) -> Option<&str> {
        if self.ephemeral.is_some() || self.through.is_some() && self.is_expiring() {
            Some(DEADLINE)
        } else {
            self.expire.as_deref()
        }
    }
    pub fn deadline(&self, row: &Value) -> Option<i64> {
        match self
            .expiry_column()
            .and_then(|field| row_image(row, self).field(field).ok().cloned())
        {
            Some(Value::Int(at)) => Some(at),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
    /// Never trust a restored effective instant; recompute from immutable capture.
    pub fn recompute_deadline(&self, row: &mut Value) -> Result<(), crate::error::RuntimeError> {
        if self.through.is_none() {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let authored = self
            .expire
            .as_ref()
            .and_then(|c| match row_image(row, self).field(c) {
                Ok(Value::Int(at)) => Some(*at),
                _ => None,
            });
        let parent = match row.field(PARENT_DEADLINE)? {
            Value::Int(at) => Some(*at),
            Value::Null => None,
            _ => {
                return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Input(
                    "captured parent deadline must be a time or null".into(),
                ));
            }
        };
        let deadline = authored
            .into_iter()
            .chain(parent)
            .min()
            .map(Value::Int)
            .unwrap_or(Value::Null);
        if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = row {
            fields.insert(DEADLINE.into(), deadline);
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl RuleExpr {
    pub fn map_fields(&self, map: &impl Fn(&str) -> String) -> Self {
        let term = |t: &RuleTerm| match t {
            RuleTerm::Field(f) => RuleTerm::Field(map(f)),
            _ => t.clone(),
        };
        match self {
            Self::Eq(a, b) => Self::Eq(term(a), term(b)),
            Self::Ne(a, b) => Self::Ne(term(a), term(b)),
            Self::And(xs) => Self::And(xs.iter().map(|r| r.map_fields(map)).collect()),
            Self::Or(xs) => Self::Or(xs.iter().map(|r| r.map_fields(map)).collect()),
            Self::Not(x) => Self::Not(Box::new(x.map_fields(map))),
            Self::Exists {
                table,
                index,
                key,
                provenance,
                equalities,
            } => Self::Exists {
                table: table.clone(),
                index: index.clone(),
                key: key.iter().map(term).collect(),
                provenance: *provenance,
                equalities: equalities
                    .iter()
                    .map(|(f, t)| (f.clone(), term(t)))
                    .collect(),
            },
            _ => self.clone(),
        }
    }
}

impl Schema {
    pub fn children(&self, parent: &str) -> impl Iterator<Item = (&String, &Table, &Through)> {
        self.tables.iter().filter_map(move |(name, t)| {
            t.through
                .as_ref()
                .filter(|c| c.parent == parent)
                .map(|c| (name, t, c))
        })
    }
    /// Stable ancestor order; admission rejects cycles before execution.
    pub fn through_order(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        fn depth(schema: &Schema, name: &str, seen: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> usize {
            if !seen.insert(name.into()) {
                return 0;
            }
            schema
                .table(name)
                .and_then(|t| t.through.as_ref())
                .map_or(0, |t| 1 + depth(schema, &t.parent, seen))
        }
        let mut names: Vec<_> = self.tables.keys().cloned().collect();
        names.sort_by_key(|name| (depth(self, name, &mut BTreeSet::new()), name.clone()));
        names
    }
}

pub fn validate(schema: &Schema) -> Vec<SchemaIssue> {
    let mut issues = Vec::new();
    for (name, table) in &schema.tables {
        let Some(t) = &table.through else {
            continue;
        };
        let mut refuse = |message: String| {
            issues.push(SchemaIssue {
                path: format!("tables.{name}.through"),
                message,
            })
        };
        let mut copy = body.to_vec(); super::intent_walk::statements(&mut copy, &mut expand_shorthand); copy
    }
    fn fact_text(value: &Expr) -> String {
        let mut value = analysis_expr(value);
        super::intent_walk::expression(&mut value, &mut |expr| {
            if is_viewer(expr) { *expr = Expr::Name { name: "viewer".into(), site: expr.site().clone() }; }
        });
        crate::print::expression(&value)
    }
    fn facts(condition: &Expr, out: &mut BTreeSet<String>) {
        if let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = condition && op == "and" { facts(left, out); facts(right, out); }
        else { out.insert(fact_text(condition)); }
    }
    fn readable(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &Schema, read: &ReadExpr, known: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
        let Some(table) = schema.table(&read.table) else { return true };
        if super::owner_equality::readable(catalog, schema, read, known) { return true; }
        let Some(info) = catalog.tables.get(&read.table) else { return table.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable(); };
        let mut pinned = BTreeMap::new();
        // Unlike older advisory lints, proof cannot guess a non-id key: typed ids
        // may select byId even beside one same-width principal/composite key.
        let selected = read.index.as_ref().and_then(|name| info.indexes.iter().find(|index| &index.name == name));
        let inferred: Vec<_> = info.indexes.iter().filter(|index| index.unique && index.is_plain() && index.columns.len() == read.keys.len()).collect();
        let positional_index = if read.index.is_some() { selected.filter(|index| index.is_plain()) }
            else { match inferred.as_slice() { [index] => Some(*index), _ => None } };
        if let Some(index) = positional_index {
            for (column, key) in index.columns.iter().zip(&read.keys) {
                pinned.insert(key.column.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| column.clone()), key.value.clone());
            }
        }
        // Extra named tests pin fields even when the positional prefix is unresolved.
        if selected.is_none_or(|index| index.computed.is_empty()) {
            for part in &read.keys { if let Some(column) = &part.column { pinned.insert(column.clone(), part.value.clone()); } }
        }
        if read.index.is_none() && read.keys.iter().filter(|key| key.column.is_none()).count() == 1 && read.keys.first().is_some_and(|part| part.column.is_none() && is_viewer(&part.value)) {
            let principal_keys: Vec<_> = table.indexes.values().filter(|index| index.unique && index.columns().len() == 1
                && table.columns.get(&index.columns()[0]) == Some(&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)).collect();
            if let [index] = principal_keys.as_slice() { pinned.insert(index.columns()[0].clone(), read.keys[0].value.clone()); }
        }
        let mut filters = Vec::new();
        if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { super::intent::split((**filter).clone(), &mut filters); }
        for filter in filters {
            if let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = &filter && op == "="
                && let Some(column) = super::intent::column(left) { pinned.entry(column.to_owned()).or_insert_with(|| (**right).clone()); }
        }
        // A total self-membership key is witnessed by the probed row itself.
        if table.rules.read.disjuncts().iter().any(|rule| {
            let RuleExpr::Exists { table: target, index, key, equalities, .. } = rule else { return false };
            target == &read.table && equalities.is_empty() && table.index(index).is_some_and(|index|
                index.unique && index.columns().len() == key.len() && index.columns().iter().zip(key).all(|(column, value)| {
                    let required = column == "id" || table.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|kind| !matches!(kind, snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Optional(_)));
                    required && (matches!(value, RuleTerm::Field(field) if field == column)
                        || matches!(value, RuleTerm::Viewer) && pinned.get(column).is_some_and(is_viewer))
                }))
        }) { return true; }
        let term = |term: &RuleTerm| -> Option<String> {
            match term {
                RuleTerm::Viewer => Some("viewer".into()),
                RuleTerm::Literal(value) => Some(RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer, RuleTerm::Literal(value.clone())).quarry().trim_start_matches("viewer = ").to_owned()),
                RuleTerm::Field(column) => pinned.get(column).map(fact_text),
                _ => None,
            }
        };
        fn holds(rule: &RuleExpr, term: &impl Fn(&RuleTerm) -> Option<String>, schema: &Schema, known: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
            match rule {
                RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Public { .. } => true,
                RuleExpr::Eq(a, b) => match (term(a), term(b)) {
                    (Some(a), Some(b)) => a == b || known.contains(&format!("({a} = {b})")) || known.contains(&format!("({b} = {a})")),
                    _ => false,
                },
                RuleExpr::And(items) => items.iter().all(|rule| holds(rule, term, schema, known)),
                RuleExpr::Or(items) => items.iter().any(|rule| holds(rule, term, schema, known)),
                RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } => {
                    let Some(target) = schema.table(table).and_then(|t| t.index(index)) else { return false };
                    let Some(key) = key.iter().map(term).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>() else { return false };
                    let plain = format!("exists {table}[{}]", key.join(", "));
                    let Some(extra) = equalities.iter().map(|(c, t)| term(t).map(|v| format!("{c} = {v}"))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>() else { return false };
                    let named = target.columns().iter().zip(&key).map(|(c, v)| format!("{c} = {v}"))
                        .chain(extra).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
                    // Positional spellings with multiple compatible index widths
                    // are not evidence for this particular raw-rule key.
                    let source = schema.table(table).unwrap();
                    let unambiguous = source.indexes.values().filter(|candidate| candidate.unique && candidate.columns().len() == key.len()).count() == 1
                        || key.len() == 1 && key[0] == "viewer" && target.columns().len() == 1
                            && source.columns.get(&target.columns()[0]) == Some(&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)
                            && source.indexes.values().filter(|candidate| candidate.unique && candidate.columns().len() == 1
                                && source.columns.get(&candidate.columns()[0]) == Some(&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)).count() == 1;

    #[cfg(feature = "server-search")]
    pub(crate) fn scan_selected(&self, _authorization: &Authorization, table: &str, name: &str, prefix: &[Value], bounds: &Bounds, dir: Dir, fields: &[String]) -> Result<Vec<(Value, Vec<u8>)>, RuntimeError> {
        self.limit(table, 1)?;
        let rows = self.raw.scan_selected(table, name, prefix, bounds, dir, fields)?;
        self.charge(rows.len());
        Ok(rows)
    }
    pub(crate) fn scan_metered(&self, _authorization: &Authorization, table: &str, name: &str, prefix: &[Value], bounds: &Bounds, dir: Dir, limit: usize, meter: &mut Meter) -> Result<Vec<(Value, Vec<u8>)>, RuntimeError> {
        let permitted = self.limit(table, limit)?;
        let rows = InternalLedger::with_meter(meter, |meter, _| {
            self.raw.scan_metered(table, name, prefix, bounds, dir, permitted, _authorization, meter)
        })?;
        self.charge(rows.len());
        if permitted < limit && rows.len() == permitted { return Err(RuntimeError::AuthorizationBudget { table: table.into(), effect: crate::schema::Effect::Read }); }
        Ok(rows)
    }
    pub(crate) fn count_prefix(&self, _authorization: &Authorization, table: &str, name: &str, prefix: &[Value], limit: u64) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        // This scalar adapter is used only by non-expiring, non-private
        // caps. It fetches no candidate rows and keeps its declared cap bound.
        self.raw.count_prefix(table, name, prefix, limit)
    }

    pub(crate) fn cap_count(&self, _authorization: &Authorization, table: &str, by: &[String], group: &[Value]) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> { self.raw.cap_count(table, by, group) }

    pub(crate) fn sequence_value(&self, _authorization: &Authorization, parent: &str, id: &str) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> { self.raw.sequence_value(parent, id) }
    pub(crate) fn provisional_sequence_floor(&self, _authorization: &Authorization, parent: &str, id: &str) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> { self.raw.provisional_sequence_floor(parent, id) }
    pub(crate) fn set_sequence(&self, parent: &str, id: &str, value: i64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> { self.raw.set_sequence(parent, id, value) }
    pub(crate) fn put(&self, name: &str, table: &Table, row: &Value, old: Option<&Value>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> { self.revision.set(self.revision.get().wrapping_add(1)); self.lookup_cache.borrow_mut().take(); self.raw.put(name, table, row, old) }
    pub(crate) fn delete(&self, name: &str, table: &Table, old: &Value) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> { self.revision.set(self.revision.get().wrapping_add(1)); self.lookup_cache.borrow_mut().take(); self.raw.delete(name, table, old)
    }
    pub(crate) fn track_time_audience(
        &self,
        name: &str,
        table: &Table,
        id: &str,
        row: Option<&Value>,
        now: i64,
        published: bool,
    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.raw.track_time_audience(name, table, id, row, now, published)
    }
    pub(crate) fn time_audience_pending(&self, name: &str, id: &str) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        self.raw.time_audience_pending(name, id) }
    pub(crate) fn time_audience_published(&self, name: &str, id: &str) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        self.raw.time_audience_published(name, id) }
    pub(crate) fn holds_assets(&self) -> bool { self.raw.holds_assets() }
    pub(crate) fn widen_change_evidence(&self) { self.raw.widen_change_evidence() }
    /// Draw a schema-fixed number of rows from the request's physical
    /// allowance up front, so later authorization work exhausts it at a point
    /// independent of the rows a delete's actions reach (LLP 3000.039 §4.2).
    pub(crate) fn draw(&self, table: &str, rows: u64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let rows = usize::try_from(rows).unwrap_or(usize::MAX);
        if self.remaining.get() < rows { self.remaining.set(0); return Err(RuntimeError::AuthorizationBudget { table: table.into(), effect: crate::schema::Effect::Delete }); }
        self.remaining.set(self.remaining.get() - rows);
        Ok(())
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.039#5.1 — reserved action hooks spend the physical draw once; ordinary root probes remain outside this scope.
    pub(super) fn reference_prepaid(&self, prepaid: bool) -> bool { self.reference_prepaid.replace(prepaid) }
    pub(crate) fn asset(&self, id: &str) -> Result<Option<Asset>, RuntimeError> { self.raw.asset(id) }
    pub(crate) fn claim_asset(&self, id: &str, by: &str, table: &str, row: &str, column: &str) -> Result<Asset, RuntimeError> { self.raw.claim_asset(id, by, table, row, column) }
    pub(crate) fn release_asset(&self, id: &str, #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")] table: &str, #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")] row_id: &str, #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")] column: &str, now: i64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> { #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")] { self.raw.release_asset(id, table, row_id, column, now) } #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))] { self.raw.release_asset(id, now) } }
    pub(crate) fn effect_once(&self, effect: &str, key: &str) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> { self.raw.effect_once(effect, key) }
    pub(crate) fn provisional_id_after(&self, table: &str, after: &str) -> Result<Option<String>, RuntimeError> { self.raw.provisional_id_after(table, after) }
    pub(crate) fn has_provisional(&self, table: &str) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> { self.raw.has_provisional(table) }
    pub(crate) fn provisional_groups(&self, table: &str, fields: &[String]) -> Result<Option<Vec<Vec<crate::value::Value>>>, RuntimeError> { self.raw.provisional_groups(table, fields) }
    pub(crate) fn provisional_row(&self, table: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> { self.raw.provisional_row(table, row) }
    pub(crate) fn search_retained_row(&self, table: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> { self.raw.search_retained_row(table, row) }
    pub(crate) fn retained_row(&self, table: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> { self.raw.retained_row(table, row) }
    pub(crate) fn retained_group(&self, table: &str, index: &str, prefix: &[Value]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> { self.raw.retained_group(table, index, prefix) }
    /// Device acquisition facts carry no row payload, like retained_row.
    pub(crate) fn current_row(&self, table: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> { self.raw.current_row(table, row) }
    pub(crate) fn current_group(&self, table: &str, index: &str, prefix: &[Value]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> { self.raw.current_group(table, index, prefix) }
    pub(crate) fn recording_covered(&self, table: &str, row: &Value, viewer: &str, now: i64, scope: &Authorization, meter: &mut Meter) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> { InternalLedger::with_meter(meter, |meter, _| {
            self.raw.recording_covered(table, row, viewer, now, scope, meter)
        }) }
    pub(crate) fn acquired_boundary(&self, table: &str, index: &str, prefix: &[Value]) -> Result<Option<AcquiredBoundary>, RuntimeError> { self.raw.acquired_boundary(table, index, prefix) }
    pub(crate) fn prefetch_enabled(&self) -> bool { self.raw.prefetch_enabled() }
    #[allow(dead_code)] // Used by the native prefetch planner; absent on web.
    pub(crate) fn prefetch(&self, requests: &[PrefetchRequest]) { self.raw.prefetch(requests) }
}

    ceilings: Ceilings,
    cut: Cut,
    authorizing: bool,
    nested: bool,
}

    fn guard(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &Schema, program: &str, condition: &Expr, known: &BTreeSet<String>, system: bool, notes: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
        if system { return; }
        let mut reads = Vec::new();
        let mut copy = analysis_expr(condition);
        super::intent_walk::expression(&mut copy, &mut |expr| {
            if let Expr::Exists { read, .. } = expr { reads.push(read.clone()); }
            if let Expr::Binary { op,left,right,.. } = expr && op == "=" {
                if let (Expr::Read(read),Expr::Literal { value:Literal::Null,.. }) | (Expr::Literal { value:Literal::Null,.. },Expr::Read(read)) = (left.as_ref(),right.as_ref()) { reads.push(read.clone()); }
            }
        });
        for read in reads {
            let Some(table) = schema.table(&read.table) else { continue };
            if readable(catalog, schema, &read, known) { continue; }
            if notes.iter().any(|note| note.site == read.site && note.code == "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" || note.site.file == read.site.file && note.site.line == read.site.line && note.code == "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE" && note.message.starts_with(&format!("{program}: this probe reads {} under", read.table))) { continue; }
            let rule_site = rule_site(catalog, &read.table, Effect::Read).map(|site| format!(" ({site})")).unwrap_or_default();
            notes.push(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE",
                format!("{program}: this probe reads {} under its read rule `{}`{rule_site}; rows hidden from the caller do not satisfy a program exists, so using this value as a negative guard cannot enforce absence across those rows (table-rule probes see all rows)", read.table, table.rules.read.quarry()),
                "use a unique/cap/after insert constraint for an invariant across hidden rows, or authorize this caller to read the rows the guard must observe; keep this probe only when caller-visible existence is intended").guide("rules"));
        }
    }
    fn absence(condition: &Expr, inverted: bool) -> Vec<ReadExpr> {
        match condition {
            Expr::Exists { read, .. } => {
                let mut out = if inverted { vec![read.clone()] } else { vec![] };
                out.extend(absence(&Expr::Read(read.clone()), false)); out
            },
            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => absence(value, !inverted),
            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "=" || op == "!=" => {
                let pair = match (left.as_ref(), right.as_ref()) {
                    (value, Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(b), .. }) | (Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(b), .. }, value) => Some((value, *b)),
                    _ => None,
                };
                pair.map(|(value, b)| absence(value, inverted ^ (op == "=" && !b || op == "!=" && b))).unwrap_or_else(|| absence(left, inverted).into_iter().chain(absence(right, inverted)).collect())
            }
            Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => absence(left, inverted).into_iter().chain(absence(right, inverted)).collect(),
            Expr::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => {
                let mut all = Vec::new(); let mut copy = (**condition).clone();
                super::intent_walk::expression(&mut copy, &mut |expr| { if let Expr::Exists { read, .. } = expr { all.push(read.clone()); } });
                all.extend(absence(then, inverted)); all.extend(absence(otherwise, inverted)); all
            }
            Expr::Unary { value, .. } | Expr::Member { base: value, .. } | Expr::Assert { value, .. }
                | Expr::ShapeUse { base: value, .. } | Expr::Walk { seed: value, .. } => absence(value, inverted),
            Expr::Array { items, .. } | Expr::Call { args: items, .. } => items.iter().flat_map(|value| absence(value, inverted)).collect(),
            Expr::Object { fields, .. } => fields.iter().filter_map(|field| field.value.as_ref()).flat_map(|value| absence(value, inverted)).collect(),
            Expr::Shape { base, fields, .. } => absence(base, inverted).into_iter().chain(fields.iter().filter_map(|field| match field {
                ShapeField::Field { value, .. } => Some(value), _ => None,
            }).flat_map(|value| absence(value, inverted))).collect(),
            Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Created { read, .. } => {
                let mut values: Vec<_> = read.keys.iter().map(|key| &key.value).chain(read.seeks.iter().map(|key| &key.value)).collect();
                values.extend(read.filter.as_deref()); values.extend(read.matching.as_deref());
                values.extend(read.range.as_ref().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_deref()));
                values.into_iter().flat_map(|value| absence(value, false)).collect()
            },
            Expr::Write { keys, fields, condition, .. } => keys.iter().map(|key| &key.value)
                .chain(fields.iter().filter_map(|field| field.value.as_ref())).chain(condition.as_deref())
                .flat_map(|value| absence(value, inverted)).collect(),
            Expr::Merge(merge) => {
                let mut values: Vec<Expr> = [&merge.matching, &merge.filter, &merge.cursor].into_iter().filter_map(|value| value.as_deref().cloned()).collect();
                for lane in &merge.lanes {
                    values.extend(lane.admission.as_deref().cloned()); values.extend(lane.distinct.clone());
                    values.extend(lane.prefix.iter().cloned()); values.extend(lane.loop_.as_ref().map(|(_, value)| value.clone()));
                    for read in lane.planned.iter().chain(lane.nulls.as_ref().map(|(_, read)| read)) { values.push(Expr::Read(*read.clone())); }
                    if let Some(fields) = &lane.project { values.extend(fields.iter().filter_map(|field| if let ShapeField::Field { value, .. } = field { Some(value.clone()) } else { None })); }
                }
                values.iter().flat_map(|value| absence(value, inverted)).collect()
            },
            Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Focal { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. } => vec![],
        }
    }
    // Reverse dependency closure identifies query values consumed by guards,
    // across aliases/objects and all possible rebinding paths. The observation
    // is checked at its original statement, with that statement's authority.
    fn has_guard(catalog: &Catalog, statements: &[Stmt], visiting: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
        if statements.iter().any(|stmt| matches!(stmt, Stmt::Require { .. } | Stmt::If { .. })) { return true; }
        let mut copy = analysis_body(statements); let mut found = false;
        super::intent_walk::statements(&mut copy, &mut |expr| {
            if matches!(expr, Expr::If { .. }) { found = true; }
            for (name, shape) in definitions(catalog, expr) {
                let key = format!("{shape}:{name}");
                if !visiting.insert(key.clone()) { found = true; continue; }
                found |= helper_body(catalog, &name, shape).is_some_and(|body| has_guard(catalog, &body, visiting));
                visiting.remove(&key);
            }
        });
        // Explicit guards can also be nested in loops/viewer scopes.
        found || statements.iter().any(|stmt| match stmt { Stmt::For { body, .. } | Stmt::As { body, .. } => has_guard(catalog, body, visiting), _ => false })
    }
    fn guarded_inputs(catalog: &Catalog, expr: &Expr) -> Vec<Expr> {
        let consumed = definitions(catalog, expr).iter().any(|(name, shape)| helper_body(catalog, name, *shape)
            .is_some_and(|body| has_guard(catalog, &body, &mut BTreeSet::from([format!("{shape}:{name}")]))));
        match expr {
            Expr::Call { args, .. } if consumed => args.clone(),
            Expr::ShapeUse { base, .. } if consumed => vec![(**base).clone()],
            Expr::Shape { base, fields, .. } => {
                let values: Vec<_> = fields.iter().filter_map(|field| if let ShapeField::Field { value, site, .. } = field { Some(Stmt::Expr { value: value.clone(), site: site.clone() }) } else { None }).collect();
                if consumed || has_guard(catalog, &values, &mut BTreeSet::new()) { vec![(**base).clone()] } else { vec![] }
            },
            _ => vec![],
        }
    }
    fn guard_bindings(catalog: &Catalog, statements: &[Stmt]) -> BTreeSet<String> {
        fn add_names(expr: &Expr, out: &mut BTreeSet<String>) {
            if let Expr::Name { name, .. } = expr { out.insert(name.clone()); }
            if let Expr::Object { fields, .. } | Expr::Write { fields, .. } = expr {
                out.extend(fields.iter().filter(|field| field.value.is_none() && !field.spread).map(|field| field.name.clone()));
            }
        }
        fn names(value: &Expr) -> BTreeSet<String> {
            let mut out = BTreeSet::new(); let mut copy = analysis_expr(value);
            super::intent_walk::expression(&mut copy, &mut |expr| add_names(expr, &mut out)); out
        }
        fn input_names(value: &Expr) -> BTreeSet<String> {
            let mut out = BTreeSet::new(); value_nodes(value, &mut |expr| add_names(expr, &mut out)); out
        }
        fn gather(catalog: &Catalog, statements: &[Stmt], guards: &mut BTreeSet<String>, edges: &mut BTreeMap<String, BTreeSet<String>>) {
            let mut copy = analysis_body(statements);
            super::intent_walk::statements(&mut copy, &mut |expr| {
                if let Expr::If { condition, .. } = expr { guards.extend(names(condition)); }
                // A definition can consume its input as a guard even when the
                // call itself is not a conditional. Conservatively track all
                // argument/base dependencies of such definitions.
                for input in guarded_inputs(catalog, expr) { guards.extend(input_names(&input)); }
            });
            for statement in statements {
                match statement {
                    Stmt::Bind { names: bindings, value, .. } => for name in bindings { edges.entry(name.clone()).or_default().extend(names(value)); },
                    Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => guards.extend(names(condition)),
                    Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body, .. } => { guards.extend(names(condition)); gather(catalog, then_body, guards, edges); gather(catalog, else_body, guards, edges); }
                    Stmt::For { name, domain, body, .. } => { edges.entry(name.clone()).or_default().extend(names(domain)); gather(catalog, body, guards, edges); }
                    Stmt::As { body, .. } => gather(catalog, body, guards, edges),
                    _ => {}
                }
            }
        }
        let mut guards = BTreeSet::new(); let mut edges = BTreeMap::new(); gather(catalog, statements, &mut guards, &mut edges);
        loop {
            let count = guards.len();
            let next: Vec<_> = guards.iter().filter_map(|name| edges.get(name)).flat_map(|values| values.iter().cloned()).collect();
            guards.extend(next); if count == guards.len() { return guards; }
        }
    }
    fn helper_body(catalog: &Catalog, name: &str, shape: bool) -> Option<Vec<Stmt>> {
        if shape {
            let decl = catalog.shapes.get(name)?;
            Some(decl.fields.iter().filter_map(|field| match field {
                ShapeField::Field { value, site, .. } => Some(Stmt::Expr { value: value.clone(), site: site.clone() }),
                ShapeField::NamedSpread { name, site } => Some(Stmt::Expr { value: Expr::ShapeUse { base: Box::new(Expr::Focal { site: site.clone() }), name: name.clone(), site: site.clone() }, site: site.clone() }),
                _ => None,
            }).collect())
        } else {
            let helper = catalog.functions.get(name)?;
            Some(match &helper.body {
                FunctionBody::Block(body) => body.clone(),
                FunctionBody::Expression(value) => vec![Stmt::Return { value: value.clone(), site: value.site().clone() }],
            })
        }
    }
    fn definitions(catalog: &Catalog, expr: &Expr) -> Vec<(String, bool)> {
        match expr {
            Expr::Call { name, .. } if catalog.functions.contains_key(name) => vec![(name.clone(), false)],
            Expr::ShapeUse { name, .. } => vec![(name.clone(), true)],
            Expr::Name { name, .. } if catalog.shapes.contains_key(name) => vec![(name.clone(), true)],
            Expr::Shape { fields, .. } => fields.iter().filter_map(|field| if let ShapeField::NamedSpread { name, .. } = field { Some((name.clone(), true)) } else { None }).collect(),
            _ => vec![],
        }
    }

//! Seeks and row-local scan predicates.
use super::*;

impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.011.000.001#4 — retain the binder's actual choice for why, never infer a new lookup or rewrite.
    pub(super) fn record_selection(&mut self, site: &str, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, result: &str, reason: &str) {
        let named = read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_some());
        let bindings = index.columns.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(position, column)| {
            let key = if named { read.keys.iter().find(|key| key.column.as_ref() == Some(column)) } else { read.keys.get(position) }?;
            Some(format!("{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value)))
        }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
        let origin = if self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())) { "synthesized; " } else { "" };
        let source = if origin.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!("; read: {}", self.catalog.read_origins.get(&read.site.to_string()).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| print::read_expr(read))) };
        let mut selection = format!("selected {} ({bindings}); result: {result}; {origin}{reason}{source}", index.name);
        if !self.variants.is_empty() {
            let when = self.variants.iter().map(|(name, present)| format!("{} {}", name.trim_start_matches("$intentPresent:").trim_start_matches("$intentSearch:"), if *present { "present" } else { "absent" })).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
            selection = format!("when {when}: {selection}");
        }
        self.selections.entry(site.into()).and_modify(|prior| { if !prior.contains(&selection) { prior.push_str("; "); prior.push_str(&selection); } }).or_insert(selection);
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#10 — a covered total unique key reads one row; additional named equalities check that row.
    pub(super) fn read_value(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, mode: ReadMode, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Expr> {
        let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
        for key in &read.keys { if let Some(field) = &key.column { super::super::row_views::predicate_field(info, field, &read.site)?; } }
        if !read.seeks.is_empty() { return Err(type_error(&read.site, "a seek needs a bounded page", "t[prefix, col >= start] first 50 by index")); }
        if read.keys.is_empty() { return Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{}[] reads nothing; a point read needs a key", read.table), format!("{}[id]", read.table))); }
        let mut point = read.clone();
        let implicit_group = mode == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.maintained.is_some() && point.index.is_none();
        if implicit_group { point.index = Some("byGroup".into()); }
        let (mut keys, index, mut selection) = self.select_index(info, &point, mode, out)?;
        super::super::point_key::check(info, &index, read, &keys.iter().map(|(column, _, ty)|
            (column.as_deref(), !ty.is_optional() && *ty != Ty::Unknown)).collect::<Vec<_>>(), false)?;
        if implicit_group { selection = "complete maintained group key".into(); }
        let extra = keys.len() > index.columns.len();
        let mut equalities = Vec::new();
        if extra {
            for (column, value, ty) in &mut keys {
                let column = column.as_ref().expect("named key");
                if column != "id" && !info.columns.contains_key(column) {
                    return Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{} has no column {column}", read.table), name_rewrite(column, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str).chain(["id"]), None)));
                }
                let expected = self.key_ty(info, &index, column);
                if !ty.comparable(&expected) { return Err(type_error(&read.site, format!("{column} is {}; {}.{column} is {}", ty.name(), read.table, expected.name()), format!("use {} for {column}", expected.name()))); }
                *value = self.snapshot(value.clone(), out);
                if !index.columns.contains(column) { equalities.push((column.clone(), value.clone())); }
            }
            keys.retain(|(column, _, _)| index.columns.contains(column.as_ref().unwrap()));
        }
        let key = self.order_keys(info, &index, keys)?;
        let location = self.site();
        self.record_selection(&location, read, &index, if mode == ReadMode::Exists { "exists" } else if info.maintained.is_some() { "scalar" } else { "row or null" }, &selection);
        self.sources.insert(location.clone(), (read.site.clone(), print::read_expr(read)));
        let mut point = Read { site: location, table: read.table.clone(), index: index.name, key, provenance: KeyProvenance::Shape };
        // @ref LLP 3000.028#3.4 — only a caller-proved existence read may use the whole owner partition.
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists && equalities.is_empty() {
            super::super::own_keys::existence(self.schema, &mut point);
        }
        if equalities.is_empty() { return Ok(if mode == ReadMode::Exists { Expr::Exists(point) } else { Expr::Get(point) }); }
        let row = self.temp("probe");
        out.push(Stmt::Let { name: row.clone(), value: Expr::Get(point) });
        let mut condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(ir::var(&row)), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) };
        // @ref LLP 3000.014#5-what-derives-from-the-capture — a denied aggregate carries a null value, not a present group.
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists && info.maintained.is_some() {
            let present = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(ir::field(ir::var(&row), "value")), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) };
            condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left: Box::new(condition), right: Box::new(present) };
        }
        for (column, value) in equalities {
            let test = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left: Box::new(ir::field(ir::var(&row), &column)), right: Box::new(value) };
            condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left: Box::new(condition), right: Box::new(test) };
        }
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists { return Ok(condition); }
        out.push(Stmt::If { condition, then_body: Vec::new(), else_body: vec![Stmt::Assign { name: row.clone(), value: Expr::Literal(Value::Null) }] });
        Ok(ir::var(&row))
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.3 — a seek constrains the first index column after the pinned prefix.
    pub(super) fn seek(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Option<ir::Range>> {
        if read.seeks.is_empty() { return Ok(None); }
        let mut range = ir::Range { gt: None, gte: None, lt: None, lte: None };
        for bound in &read.seeks {
            if index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) != Some(&bound.column) || read.matching.is_some() {
                return Err(type_error(&bound.site, "a seek compares the first unpinned column of a plain index", "t[prefix, col >= start, col < end] first 50 by index"));
            }
            let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, &bound.column);
            let (value, ty) = self.field_value(&ObjectField { spread: false, name: bound.column.clone(), value: Some(bound.value.clone()), site: bound.site.clone() }, &expected, out)?;
            if !ty.comparable(&expected) || ty.is_optional() {
                let endpoint = print::expression(&bound.value);
                let rewrite = if ty.is_optional() {
                    format!("put the read inside `if {endpoint} != null:`, then `{} where .{} {} {endpoint}`", read.table, bound.column, bound.op)
                } else {
                    format!("use a present {} endpoint in `{} where .{} {} {endpoint}`", expected.required().name(), read.table, bound.column, bound.op)
                };
                return Err(type_error(&bound.site, format!("seek on {}.{} expects present {}, got {}", read.table, bound.column, expected.required().name(), ty.name()), rewrite));
            }
            let slot = match bound.op.as_str() { ">" => &mut range.gt, ">=" => &mut range.gte, "<" => &mut range.lt, "<=" => &mut range.lte, _ => unreachable!("parser seek comparison") };
            if slot.is_some() { return Err(type_error(&bound.site, "a seek bound is repeated", "write one lower and one upper bound")); }
            *slot = Some(Box::new(self.snapshot(value, out)));
        }
        if (range.gt.is_some() && range.gte.is_some()) || (range.lt.is_some() && range.lte.is_some()) {
            return Err(type_error(&read.site, "a seek has at most one lower and one upper bound", "t[prefix, col >= start, col < end] first 50 by index"));
        }
        // Null sorts below every present value. An upper-only nullable seek
        // must skip the entire null segment, including its id suffixes.
        if range.gt.is_none() && range.gte.is_none()
            && read.seeks.first().is_some_and(|bound| self.key_ty(info, index, &bound.column).is_optional()) {
            range.gt = Some(Box::new(ir::lit(Value::Null)));
        }
        Ok(Some(range))
    }
}

                    }
                    ("+", Ty::Time, Ty::Time) => {

                    (_, Ty::Money(c, a), Ty::Money(d, b)) if a == b && c == d && op != "*" => Ty::Money(c.clone(), *a),
                    ("*", Ty::Decimal(a), Ty::Int) | ("*", Ty::Int, Ty::Decimal(a)) => Ty::Decimal(*a),
                    ("*", Ty::Money(c, a), Ty::Int) | ("*", Ty::Int, Ty::Money(c, a)) => Ty::Money(c.clone(), *a),

                    _ => return Err(type_error(site, format!("{op} needs matching numeric types; got {} and {}", lt.name(), rt.name()), "convert one operand")),
                };

                    "+" => BinaryOp::Add,
                    "-" => BinaryOp::Sub,
                    _ => BinaryOp::Mul,
                };
                return Ok((Expr::Binary { op: ir_op, left: Box::new(l), right: Box::new(r) }, result));
            }
            other => return Err(unknown(site, format!("unknown operator {other}"), "use =, !=, <, <=, >, >=, +, -, *, and, or, ??")),
        };

        if matches!(ir_op, BinaryOp::Lt | BinaryOp::Lte | BinaryOp::Gt | BinaryOp::Gte) {
            if nullable_left { present.push(l.clone()); }
            if nullable_right { present.push(r.clone()); }

        let mut comparison = Expr::Binary { op: ir_op, left: Box::new(l), right: Box::new(r) };
        // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.1 — predicate nulls do not enter ordering;
        // the ordinary interpreter's total value ordering remains unchanged.
        for value in present.into_iter().rev() {

                left: Box::new(Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(value), right: Box::new(ir::lit(Value::Null)) }),
                right: Box::new(comparison) };
        }
        Ok((comparison, Ty::Bool))
    }

    /// An expression in an identity position: a text literal becomes an id.
    pub(super) fn expr_as_identity(&mut self, expr: &A, out: &mut setup::Body, expected: &Ty) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        if let A::Literal { value: Literal::Str(text), .. } = expr {
            if text.is_empty() {
                return Err(type_error(expr.site(), "an empty text literal cannot be an id", "use a non-empty id literal, or null"));
            }
            return Ok((Expr::Literal(Value::Id(text.clone())), expected.required()));
        }
        self.expr(expr, out)
    }

//! The Lean model's case format (LLP 3000.035 §5.2): a checked Quarry
//! program, translated from its **surface** syntax tree into the terms of
//! `snapback4/lean/Quarry/Syntax.lean`, as the JSON that
//! `snapback4/lean/Quarry/CaseJson.lean` decodes (format:
//! `snapback4/lean/README.md`, "The case format").
//!
//! **Why the surface tree.** The model is a second implementation of the
//! binder and the interpreter together. Reading the binder's IR would copy
//! the binder's decisions into the model's input, and the comparison could
//! no longer find a binder defect. So nothing here reads `bind` or the IR:
//! the input is the parsed [`Document`]s, and the only binder fact used is
//! that [`crate::compile`] reported no diagnostics, i.e. that the program
//! type-checks ([`lean`] refuses otherwise).
//!
//! **Types.** The few places that need a type (a text literal beside an id is
//! an id, OPEN V1; a positional key chooses a unique key by its argument
//! types, indexes card) take it from the surface's own declarations, which
//! the type checker has accepted: column types, argument types, helper
//! parameters, `viewer : principal`, and literal kinds, propagated through
//! locals by the small inference in [`Scope::ty`]. When that inference cannot
//! decide, the program is unsupported, never guessed.
//!
//! **The expansions** are those `Syntax.lean` documents: `public` is
//! `read <- allow`; an omitted rule is `deny`; an unnamed `update <- R` is
//! `R[old] and R[next]` (OPEN R2); positional keys become named equalities
//! on `id` (an `id`-typed or `t`-typed single key) or on the one declared
//! unique key whose column types accept the arguments in order; in a
//! `where`, each `.col = e` conjunct (e not reading the row) is a pin and the
//! remaining conjuncts are the filter; `return insert …` is a bound write
//! followed by `return`.
//!
//! **Erased, with the reason.** `sync …` and `online only` (residency
//! decides what a device holds, never a server outcome) and non-unique
//! `by` indexes (tuning; reads choose indexes themselves). A `text <= N`
//! bound is not modelled as a value restriction (OPEN W6):
//! [`LeanModule::store`] and [`LeanModule::tag_args`] refuse inputs that exceed
//! it, and a literal written past it is unsupported. The surface emitter does
//! use the bound to keep PAGE cases with nonportable synthesized keys out of
//! the fragment before the oracle. A computed text written to a bounded column
//! is not checked.
//!
//! **Everything else** outside the model's core fragment is an
//! [`Unsupported`] with a stable `reason` (counted by the harness, never a
//! pass) and a `detail` naming the construct. A translation is all or
//! nothing: there is no partial program.
// @ref LLP 3000.035#52-stage-2-a-small-lean-core — the emitter half of the Lean oracle; surface tree in, model terms out

use crate::ast::{self, Document, Effect, Expr, KeyPart, Literal, ObjectField, OnDelete, Order, ReadExpr, Stmt, TypeKind, WriteOp};
use serde_json::{Map, Value as Json, json};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

/// A construct outside the model's core fragment.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, serde::Serialize)]
pub struct Unsupported {
    /// Stable, low-cardinality name (the harness counts by it).
    pub reason: String,
    /// The construct, for a human.
    pub detail: String,
}

impl Unsupported {
    fn new(reason: impl Into<String>, detail: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self { reason: reason.into(), detail: detail.into() }
    }
}

impl std::fmt::Display for Unsupported {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}: {}", self.reason, self.detail)
    }
}

mod pages;

type R<T> = Result<T, Unsupported>;

/// The calendar builtins the model evaluates (LLP 3000.040 §2.5).
const CALENDAR: [&str; 7] = ["day", "hour", "bucket", "month", "year", "monthStart", "yearStart"];

fn no<T>(reason: &str, detail: impl Into<String>) -> R<T> {
    Err(Unsupported::new(reason, detail))
}

/// What an id-valued column or argument names. All three are the model's
/// `id` scalar; the distinction only chooses positional keys.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum IdSort {
    Plain,
    Principal,
    Ref(String),
}

/// A scalar kind of the model.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum Kind {
    Int,
    Text(Option<u32>),
    Id(IdSort),
    Bool,
    Cursor,
}

impl Kind {
    fn of(t: &TypeKind) -> R<Kind> {

            TypeKind::Int | TypeKind::Time => Kind::Int,
            // @ref LLP 3000.040#18-the-lean-model — slot bounds sit outside the model's fragment, as text bounds do.
            TypeKind::IntRange { .. } => return no("int-range", "an int range"),
            TypeKind::Text { min: None, max, format: None } => Kind::Text(Some(*max)),
            TypeKind::Text { format: Some(_), .. } => return no("text-format", "a text format"),

        match e {

            Expr::Name { name, .. } => self.vars.get(name).cloned().unwrap_or(Ty::Unknown),
            Expr::Literal { value, .. } => match literal_scalar(value) {
                Ok((_, Some(k))) => Ty::Scalar(k),
                _ => Ty::Unknown,
            },
            Expr::Member { base, name, .. } => {

                        _ => None,

            Expr::Read(read) if read.range.is_none() && read.filter.is_none() && !read.keys.is_empty() => Ty::Row(read.table.clone()),
            Expr::Call { name, .. } if name == "count" || CALENDAR.contains(&name.as_str()) => Ty::Scalar(Kind::Int),
            Expr::Exists { .. } => Ty::Scalar(Kind::Bool),
            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } => if op == "not" { Ty::Scalar(Kind::Bool) } else { self.ty(value) },
            Expr::Binary { op, left, .. } => match op.as_str() {
                "+" | "-" | "*" | "/" | "%" | "??" => self.ty(left),
                _ => Ty::Scalar(Kind::Bool),
            },
            Expr::Assert { value, .. } => self.ty(value),
            Expr::If { then, .. } => self.ty(then),
            _ => Ty::Unknown,
        }
    }

    fn kind(&self, e: &Expr) -> Option<Kind> {
        match self.ty(e) {
            Ty::Scalar(k) => Some(k),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

+#[test]
+fn hidden_query_count_is_dev_console_only() {
+ let project=Project::new();fs::write(project.0.join("snapback/schema.q"),format!("{SOURCE}\nquery page(c: cursor ?):\n  return rows first 10 by .id after c\n")).unwrap();
+ let dev=Host::start(project,true,false);
+ let seed=dev.post("/seed",json!([{"table":"rows","row":{"id":"hidden","owner":"dev:bob"}}]));assert_eq!(seed["seeded"],1);
+ let reply=dev.post("/q/page",json!({"args":{"c":null}}));assert_eq!(reply["data"],json!([]));assert!(!reply.to_string().contains("hidden"));
+ let until=Instant::now()+Duration::from_secs(5);
+ loop {let logs=fs::read_to_string(&dev.stderr).unwrap();if logs.contains("dev query: page hidden: 1") {break;}assert!(Instant::now()<until,"{logs}");std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10));}
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn bare_persona_arguments_refuse_only_on_the_dev_door() {
+ let dev=Host::start(Project::new(),true,false);
+ let bare=dev.request("add","alice","x-snapback-persona: alice");assert_eq!(bare["why"]["code"],"E_INPUT","{bare}");assert!(bare["why"]["message"].as_str().unwrap().contains("dev:alice"));
+ assert_eq!(dev.request("add","dev:alice","x-snapback-persona: alice")["state"],"sent");
+ let prod=Host::start(Project::new(),false,false);
+ let minted=Command::new(BIN).current_dir(&prod.project.0).args(["token","--service","mp-test","--release","release"]).output().unwrap();assert!(minted.status.success());
+ let token=String::from_utf8(minted.stdout).unwrap();
+ let raw=prod.request("add","alice",&format!("Authorization: Bearer {}",token.trim()));assert_eq!(raw["why"]["code"],"E_RULE","production must apply its table rule, not dev persona validation: {raw}");
+}
+#[test]
+fn fixture_queries_print_hidden_counts_and_persona_arguments_have_the_same_guard() {
+ let project=Project::new();fs::write(project.0.join("snapback/schema.q"),format!("{SOURCE}query point(id: rows):\n  return rows[id]\n")).unwrap();
+ let fixture=project.0.join("fixture");fs::create_dir(&fixture).unwrap();
+ fs::write(fixture.join("rows.jsonl"),"{\"id\":\"hidden\",\"owner\":\"dev:bob\"}\n").unwrap();
+ fs::write(fixture.join("test.cases.jsonl"),"{\"as\":\"alice\",\"op\":\"point\",\"args\":{\"id\":\"hidden\"},\"expect\":\"complete\",\"data\":null}\n{\"as\":\"alice\",\"op\":\"add\",\"args\":{\"owner\":\"alice\"},\"expect\":\"E_INPUT\"}\n").unwrap();
+ let out=Command::new(BIN).current_dir(&project.0).args(["test","--fixture","fixture"]).output().unwrap();assert!(out.status.success(),"{out:?}");let text=String::from_utf8(out.stdout).unwrap();assert!(text.contains("hidden: 1"),"{text}");assert!(text.contains("E_INPUT"),"{text}");
+}
+impl Host {
+ fn post_authenticated(&self,path:&str,payload:Value,auth:&str)->Value {
+  let body=payload.to_string();let mut socket=TcpStream::connect(&self.address).unwrap();socket.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(15))).unwrap();
+  write!(socket,"POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\n{auth}\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{body}",body.len()).unwrap();let mut reply=String::new();socket.read_to_string(&mut reply).unwrap();serde_json::from_str(reply.split_once("\r\n\r\n").unwrap().1).unwrap()
+ }
+}
+#[test]
+fn ephemeral_and_action_http_doors_apply_dev_principal_guards_and_seat_clauses() {
+ const SOURCE:&str="use server\nuse personas alice\ntable typing:\n  owner: principal\n  unique owner\n  cap 50\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  ephemeral 4s\nmutation add(owner: principal):\n  return insert typing {owner}\naction notify(owner: principal)\n";
+ let project=Project::new();fs::write(project.0.join("snapback/schema.q"),SOURCE).unwrap();let dev=Host::start(project,true,false);
+ let bare=dev.request("add","alice","x-snapback-persona: alice");assert_eq!(bare["why"]["code"],"E_INPUT","{bare}");assert!(bare["why"]["message"].as_str().unwrap().contains("dev:alice"));
+ let bare_action=dev.post_authenticated("/a/notify?wait=0",json!({"id":"bare-action","args":{"owner":"alice"}}),"x-snapback-persona: alice");assert_eq!(bare_action["denied"]["code"],"E_INPUT","{bare_action}");
+ let valid=dev.post_authenticated("/a/notify?wait=0",json!({"id":"valid-action","args":{"owner":"dev:alice"}}),"x-snapback-persona: alice");assert_eq!(valid["state"],"pending","{valid}");
+ let project=Project::new();fs::write(project.0.join("snapback/schema.q"),SOURCE).unwrap();let prod=Host::start(project,false,false);
+ let minted=Command::new(BIN).current_dir(&prod.project.0).args(["token","--service","mp-test","--release","release"]).output().unwrap();assert!(minted.status.success());let token=String::from_utf8(minted.stdout).unwrap();let auth=format!("Authorization: Bearer {}",token.trim());
+ let denied=prod.request("add","alice",&auth);assert_eq!(denied["why"]["code"],"E_RULE","{denied}");assert!(denied["why"]["message"].as_str().unwrap().ends_with("typing rule clause: insert <- .owner = viewer"),"{denied}");
+ let action=prod.post_authenticated("/a/notify?wait=0",json!({"id":"prod-action","args":{"owner":"alice"}}),&auth);assert_eq!(action["state"],"pending","{action}");
+}

#[test]
fn live_reference_predicate_emits_an_authorized_point_read_before_the_field() {
    let m = module("table tags:\n  rank: int\n  read <- allow\n  online only\ntable items:\n  tag: tags\n  read <- allow\n  online only\nquery q():\n  return items where .tag.rank = 1 first 2 by .id\n");
    let b = body(&m, "q");
    assert_eq!(b[0]["ret"]["page"]["filter"], json!({"bin":["eq",{"field":[{"point":{"table":"tags","key":[["id",{"dot":"tag"}]]}},"rank"]},{"lit":{"int":"1"}}]}));
}

#[test]
fn a_helper_reference_with_unknown_return_type_is_named_unsupported() {
    let m = module("table tags:\n  rank: int\n  read <- allow\n  online only\nfn same(x: tags): x\nquery q(t: tags):\n  return same(t).rank\n");
    assert_eq!(reason(&m, "q"), "member-type");
}

#[test]
fn page3_four_variant_non_null_enum_is_named_unsupported_at_schema_translation() {
    let source = "table docs:\n  owner: principal\n  species: enum('a', 'b', 'c', 'd') ?\n  at: int\n  read <- .owner = viewer or .species != null\n  online only\nquery docs(c: cursor ?):\n  return docs where .species != null first 1 by .at after c\n";
    let Err(LeanError::Unsupported(unsupported)) = lean(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]) else { panic!("enum must not be certified Supported") };
    assert_eq!(unsupported.reason, "type-enum");
}
#[test]
fn page3_null_exempt_cap_cannot_supply_an_implicit_model_page() {
    let source = "table groups:\n  public\n  online only\ntable docs:\n  group: groups ? on delete set null\n  owner: principal\n  helper: principal\n  cap 5 by group\n  read <- .owner = viewer or .helper = viewer\n  online only\nquery docs(g: groups ?):\n  return docs where .group = g\n";
    let Err(LeanError::Diagnostics(diagnostics)) = lean(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]) else { panic!("checked path must reject the set-null cap") };
    assert!(diagnostics.iter().any(|diagnostic| diagnostic.starts_with("E_REFERENCE_ACTION:") || diagnostic.starts_with("E_QUARRY_BOUND:")), "{diagnostics:?}");
    let (document, errors) = snapback4_lang::parse_document("schema.q", source);
    assert!(errors.is_empty());
    let unchecked = snapback4_lang::emit::lean::from_documents(&[document]).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(unchecked.program("docs").unwrap_err().reason, "read-unbounded");
}

#[test]
fn contextual_reference_projection_is_independent_and_unresolved_helpers_are_named(){
 let source="table parents:\n  name: text <=20\n  public\n  insert <- allow\ntable kids:\n  parent: parents on delete keep\n  public\n  insert <- allow\nmutation add(id: id):\n  parent = insert parents {id,name:'parent'}\n  insert kids {id:'child',parent}\n  return parent.id\n";
 let implicit=body(&module(source),"add");
 let explicit=body(&module(&source.replace("id:'child',parent}","id:'child',parent:parent.id}")),"add");
 assert_eq!(implicit,explicit);
 let helper=source.replace("mutation add(id: id):","fn same(p: parents): p\nmutation add(id: id):").replace("id:'child',parent}","id:'child',parent:same(parent)}");
 assert_eq!(reason(&module(&helper),"add"),"reference-expression-type");
}

    fn select_index(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, mode: ReadMode, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Vec<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>, ast::IndexDecl, String)> {
        // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — retain bound keys for type selection and final lowering.
        // No cloned expression pass: nested lookups must not multiply binder work.
        let mut keys = Vec::<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>::new();
        let mixed = read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_some()) && read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_none());
        let types = if mixed {
            let prefix = read.keys.iter().take_while(|key| key.column.is_none()).count();
            self.bind_key_parts(info, read, prefix, &mut keys, out)?;
            Some(keys.iter().map(|(_, expr, ty)| (ty.clone(), matches!(expr, Expr::Literal(Value::String(_))))).collect::<Vec<_>>())
        } else { None };
        let mut normalized = super::mixed_keys::read_typed(info, read, self.catalog, types.as_deref())?;
        self.name_bound_keys(info, &normalized, &mut keys)?;
        let read = normalized.as_ref();
        super::validate_key_names(read)?;
        let mut positional_reason = None;
        if !read.keys.is_empty() && read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_none()) {
            let declared = |index: &&ast::IndexDecl| !self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone()));
            let mut candidates = info.indexes.iter().filter(declared).filter(|index| match &read.index {
                Some(name) => &index.name == name,
                None => index.unique && index.columns.len() == read.keys.len(),
            }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
            if read.index.is_none() && candidates.len() > 1 {
                self.bind_key_parts(info, read, read.keys.len(), &mut keys, out)?;
                let types = keys.iter().map(|(_, expr, ty)| (ty.clone(), matches!(expr, Expr::Literal(Value::String(_))))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                candidates = super::positional_keys::candidates(info, read, &types, &self.catalog.synthesized);
            }
            if read.index.is_none() && mode == ReadMode::Range && read.order_keys.is_empty() && candidates.len() == 1 {
                let scan = info.indexes.iter().filter(declared).find(|index| !index.unique && index.is_plain() && index.columns.len() > read.keys.len());
                let columns = scan.map(|index| index.columns.as_slice()).unwrap_or(&candidates[0].columns);
                let predicate = read.keys.iter().zip(columns).map(|(key, column)| format!(".{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
                return Err(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN", "a positional key selects one declared unique row, not a scan prefix",
                    format!("{} where {predicate} first 20 by .id", read.table)).guide("reads"));
            }
            if candidates.len() != 1 {
                return Err(super::positional_keys::refusal(info, read, &candidates, self.catalog));
            }
            if read.index.is_none() {
                positional_reason = Some(if info.indexes.iter().filter(declared).filter(|index| index.unique && index.columns.len() == read.keys.len()).count() == 1 {
                    "only declared unique key of this arity"
                } else { "unique positional key selected by argument types" });
                normalized.to_mut().index = Some(candidates[0].name.clone());
            }
        }
        let read = normalized.as_ref();
        let point = mode != ReadMode::Range;
        self.bind_key_parts(info, read, read.keys.len(), &mut keys, out)?;
        let named = keys.iter().all(|(c, _, _)| c.is_some()) && !keys.is_empty();
        let mut selection = if read.index.is_some() { "explicit by" } else if named { "named equality columns" } else { "compatible positional key types" };
        // A `words(..)` index serves one read: `matching` with exactly its
        // leading columns as keys. Every other read sees plain indexes only.
        let searching = mode == ReadMode::Range && read.matching.is_some();
        let mut candidates: Vec<&ast::IndexDecl> = info
            .indexes
            .iter()
            .filter(|index| read.index.as_ref().is_none_or(|explicit| explicit == &index.name))
            .filter(|index| read.seeks.iter().all(|b| index.columns.get(keys.len()) == Some(&b.column)))
            .filter(|index| if searching { index.searches_after(keys.len()) } else { index.words.is_none() })
            .filter(|index| match mode {
                ReadMode::Point | ReadMode::FilteredPoint => index.unique && (index.columns.len() == keys.len() || (named && mode == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.total_unique(index) && index.columns.len() < keys.len())),
                ReadMode::Exists => (index.columns.len() == keys.len() && (named || read.index.is_some() || index.unique)) || (named && info.total_unique(index) && index.columns.len() < keys.len()),
                ReadMode::Range => index.columns.len() > keys.len() || (!read.order_keys.is_empty() && index.unique && index.columns.len() == keys.len()),
            })
            .filter(|index| {
                if named {
                    if index.columns.len() < keys.len() { index.columns.iter().all(|column| keys.iter().any(|(c, _, _)| c.as_ref() == Some(column))) } else { keys.iter().all(|(c, _, _)| index.columns[..keys.len()].contains(c.as_ref().unwrap())) }
                } else {
                    true
                }
            })
            .collect();
        if named && matches!(mode, ReadMode::FilteredPoint | ReadMode::Exists) {
            if let Some(width) = candidates.iter().filter(|i| info.total_unique(i)).map(|i| i.columns.len()).min() {
                candidates.retain(|i| info.total_unique(i) && i.columns.len() == width);
                // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.7 — named equalities covering two total unique keys
                // (`loans[borrower = viewer, telescope = t]`, lane 057) test one row either
                // way, the other key's columns being extra equalities on it; the first
                // declared key decides, as a rule probe already does (§11).
                // Same-column keys in two orders (`unique pair: low, high` and `unique
                // inverse: high, low`) still need an explicit `by` (guide indexes).
                let mut sets = candidates.iter().map(|index| { let mut columns = index.columns.clone(); columns.sort(); columns }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                sets.dedup();
                if candidates.len() > 1 && sets.len() == candidates.len() {
                    selection = "first declared unique key the named equalities cover";
                    candidates.truncate(1);
                }
            } else if candidates.iter().any(|i| i.unique) {
                // Exact nullable unique keys still select points. A competing
                // nonunique index must not turn an exists probe into a tie.
                candidates.retain(|i| i.unique);
            }
        }
        // Positional keys select by type; a text literal may meet an
        // identity column (it becomes an id once the index is chosen).
        if !named {
            candidates.retain(|index| {
                keys.iter().enumerate().all(|(position, (_, e, t))| {
                let names: Vec<&str> = candidates.iter().map(|i| i.name.as_str()).collect();
                let changes = candidates.iter().filter(|index| !self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())))
                    .map(|index| crate::repair::ReadChange::Index(index.name.clone())).collect();
                Err(ambiguous(&read.site, format!("{} has {} indexes matching this key: {}; choose an index valid for this read and its arguments", read.table, names.len(), names.join(", ")), "")
                    .repair_read(&read.site, changes))

    }

    fn key_ty(&self, info: &TableInfo, index: &ast::IndexDecl, column: &str) -> Ty {
        if index.computed.contains_key(column) { return Ty::Int; }
        if column == "id" {
            return Ty::Ref(info.decl.name.clone());
        }
        info.columns.get(column).map(Ty::from_scalar).unwrap_or(Ty::Unknown)
    }

    fn order_keys(&mut self, info: &TableInfo, index: &ast::IndexDecl, keys: Vec<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>) -> Fallible<Vec<Expr>> {
        let named = keys.iter().all(|(c, _, _)| c.is_some());
        if !named {
            for (position, (_, _, t)) in keys.iter().enumerate() {
                let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, &index.columns[position]);
                if !t.comparable(&expected) {
                    return Err(type_error(&index.site, format!("key {} is {}; {}.{}.{} is {}", position + 1, t.name(), info.decl.name, index.name, index.columns[position], expected.name()), format!("use {} for key {}", expected.name(), position + 1)));
                }
            }
            return Ok(keys.into_iter().map(|(_, e, _)| e).collect());
        }
        let mut ordered = Vec::new();
        for column in index.columns.iter().take(keys.len()) {
            let Some(position) = keys.iter().position(|(c, _, _)| c.as_deref() == Some(column)) else {
                return Err(unknown(&index.site, format!("key omits column {column} of {}.{}", info.decl.name, index.name), format!("name each prefix column once: {}", index.columns.iter().take(keys.len()).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))));
            };
            let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, column);
            if !keys[position].2.comparable(&expected) {
                return Err(type_error(&index.site, format!("{column} is {}; {}.{column} is {}", keys[position].2.name(), info.decl.name, expected.name()), format!("use {} for {column}", expected.name())));
            }
            ordered.push(keys[position].1.clone());
        }
        Ok(ordered)
    }
//! Lexical statement scopes, flow refinements and helper expansion.

use super::*;

impl Binder<'_> {
    // ----- statements -----

    pub(super) fn block(&mut self, statements: &[S], out: &mut setup::Body, completes: bool) -> Fallible<()> {
        let before = self.visible_bindings();
        self.push_scope();
        let count = statements.len();
        let mut result = Ok(());
        for (index, statement) in statements.iter().enumerate() {
            if let Err(error) = self.statement(statement, out, completes && index + 1 == count) {
                if result.is_ok() { result = Err(error); }
            }
        }
        let after = self.visible_bindings();
        self.pop_scope();
        for (name, mut old) in before {
            let new = &after[&name];
            if old.runtime == new.runtime && old.revision != new.revision {
                self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name, new.clone());
            } else if old.runtime == new.runtime {
                // Facts added inside a lexical block remain local, while a
                // write's invalidation must reach the enclosing loop header.
                let surviving_fields: BTreeMap<_, _> = old.fields.iter()
                    .filter(|(path, ty)| new.fields.get(*path) == Some(*ty))
                    .map(|(path, ty)| (path.clone(), ty.clone()))
                    .collect();
                if surviving_fields.len() != old.fields.len() {
                    old.fields = surviving_fields;
                    self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name, old);
                }
            }
        }
        result
    }

    pub(super) fn append_errors(&mut self, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
        for error in self.errors.drain(..) {
            if !diagnostics.contains(&error) { diagnostics.push(error); }
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn record_error(&mut self, error: Diagnostic) {
        if !self.errors.contains(&error) { self.errors.push(error); }
    }

    pub(super) fn statement(&mut self, statement: &S, out: &mut setup::Body, completes: bool) -> Fallible<()> {
        let result = self.statement_inner(statement, out, completes);
        if let Err(error) = &result {
            self.record_error(error.clone());
            if let S::Bind { names, .. } = statement {
                for name in names {
                    if self.binding(name).is_some_and(|binding| binding.parameter) { continue; }
                    let runtime = self.declare(name, Ty::Unknown);
                    let binding = self.scopes.last_mut().unwrap().get_mut(name).unwrap();
                    binding.error = Some(error.clone());
                    out.push(Stmt::Let { name: runtime, value: Expr::Literal(Value::Null) });
                }
            }
        }
        result
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.1 — recursive growth is an assignment failure;
    // an ill-typed value keeps its original expression diagnostic.
    fn assignment_type_error(&self, error: Diagnostic, name: &str, value: &A, site: &Site) -> Diagnostic {
        if self.loops > 0 && error.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE" && recursive_concat(value, name) {
            recursive_list_error(site, name)
        } else { error }
    }

    fn statement_inner(&mut self, statement: &S, out: &mut setup::Body, completes: bool) -> Fallible<()> {
        match statement {
            S::As { principal, body, site } => self.as_viewer(principal, body, site, out),
            S::Return { value, site } => {

                let prior = std::mem::replace(&mut self.returned_data, self.depth == 0 && self.kind == ProgramKind::Query);
                let result = self.value_of(value, out);
                self.returned_data = prior;
                let (expr, ty) = result?;
                self.returns.push((ty, value.clone()));
                out.push(Stmt::Return(expr));

            }
            S::Bind { names, value, site } => {
                if names.len() == 2 {
                    return self.tuple_bind(names, value, site, out);
                }
                if names.len() != 1 {
                    return Err(unknown(site, "tuple bindings take exactly two names and sorted(a, b) or divmod(a, b)", "(quotient, remainder) = divmod(dividend, divisor)"));
                }

                    && let A::Array { items, .. } = right.as_ref()
                    && items.len() == 1

                    let (element, ty) = self.value_of(&items[0], out)?;

                    origins.push((Ty::List(Box::new(ty.clone()), 1), value.clone()));
                    let joined = if *max == 0 { ty.clone() } else { item.join_item(&ty).map_err(|error| self.assignment_type_error(self.join_error_many(error.at("[]"), &origins), name, value, site))? };
                    if !ty.assignable_to(&joined) || !item.assignable_to(&joined) {

                    }

                    binding.fields.clear();
                    binding.error = None;

                    self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name.clone(), binding);
                    return Ok(());
                }
